CellAlgoSwitch

+-NE
  +-eNodeBFunction
    +-Cell
      +-CellAlgoSwitch

Description

The CellAlgoSwitch MO consists of the following cell-level switches: RACH algorithm switch, SRS algorithm switch, PUCCH algorithm switch, AQM algorithm switch, CQI adjustment algorithm switch, RLF detection algorithm switch, dynamic voltage adjustment algorithm switch, RAC algorithm switch, load balancing algorithm switch, DL power control algorithm switch, UL power control algorithm switch, beamforming algorithm switch, DL scheduling switch, UL scheduling switch, RAN sharing mode switch, frequency-priority-based handover switch, multi-user beamforming algorithm switch, SSRD algorithm switch, distance-based handover switch, access barring algorithm switch, eICIC switch, CRS interference cancellation algorithm switch, UL CoMP algorithm switch, DL CoMP algorithm switch, and MRC/IRC adaptation switch.

Effective Scenario

Immediately

Relations Between MOs

  • Possible parents:

    Cell
  • Relation with the CellMLB and CellShutdown MOs:
    • When option InterFreqMlbSwitch or InterFreqBlindMlbSwitch of MlbAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is selected, MlbTriggerMode in CellMLB is set to PRB_ONLY or PRB_OR_UE_NUMBER and CellShutdownSwitch in CellShutdown is set to a value other than OFF, the sum of InterFreqMlbThd and LoadOffset in the CellMLB MO must be greater than that of DlPrbThd and DlPrbOffset in the CellShutdown MO. In addition, the sum of InterFreqMlbThd and LoadOffset in the CellMLB MO must be greater than that of UlPrbThd and UlPrbOffset in the CellShutdown MO.
    • When option InterFreqMlbSwitch or InterFreqBlindMlbSwitch of MlbAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is selected, MlbTriggerMode in CellMLB is set to PRB_ONLY or PRB_OR_UE_NUMBER and CellShutdownSwitch in CellShutdown is set to a value other than OFF, InterFreqMlbThd in the CellMLB MO must be greater than DlPrbThd in the CellShutdown MO. In addition, InterFreqMlbThd in the CellMLB MO must be greater than UlPrbThd in the CellShutdown MO.
  • Relation with the Cell MO:
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch and BfAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set OFF.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and MultiRruCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO is set to DIGITAL_COMBINATION, UlJointReceptionSwitch under UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • The SFNPDCCHDCSSWITCH option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter is set to ADAPTIVE.
    • DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd is CELL_TDD Model and DlBandWidth is CELL_BW_N25, DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be set OFF.
    • PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to On only if the FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 1T2R or 2T2R.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 2T8R, UlVmimoSwitch under UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 2T8R, DlCompSwitch under DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, then the AntCalibrationAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch could not turn on CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch.
    • AntCalibrationAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch could turn on CrossPolaCalibrationSwitch only if TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T4R.
    • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if SmartIrcSwitch under PuschIrcAlgoSwitch is turned on, FddTddInd must be set to TDD.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch or TM10Switch, DlCyclicPrefix must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, DlBandWidth must be set to CELL_BW_N50 or CELL_BW_N75 orCELL_BW_N100.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, SubframeAssignment must be set to SA1 or SA2 or SA5.
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and in a CellAlgoSwitch, and EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, then TxRxMode is 4T4R or TxRxMode is 8T8R or TxRxMode is 64T64R or TxRxMode is 32T32R, BfAlgoSwitch must be set to turn on BfSwitch.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if EnhMIMOSwitch turn on TM9Switch, HighSpeedFlag must be set to LOW_SPEED or HIGH_SPEED.
    • In a Cell, only when HighSpeedFlag is set to LOW_SPEED, IntraBaseBandSwitch and InterBaseBandSwitch under SfnUplinkCompSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch can be configured to on.
    • If the TM9Switch or TM10Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, CrsPortNum in the Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4 (valid only for FDD), CRS_PORT_2, or CRS_PORT_1.
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, then the SfnPdcchSdmaSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T8R, UlVmimoSwitch under UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If TxRxMode in a Cell MO is set to 4T8R, DlCompSwitch under DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • In a Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option can be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch, the EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to STATIC.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the BfSwitch option of BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_COMBINATION.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO cannot be set to DYNAMIC.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the DlBandWidth parameter in the Cell MO must be set to a value greater than CELL_BW_N25.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch, the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • If the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP, the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If the DlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP, the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be turned off.
    • If HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If SectorEqmNum in a Cell MO is set to smaller than 3, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, SFN, respectively.
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON and MultiRruCellFlag in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode in this Cell MO must be set to SFN.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m, UlJointReceptionSwitch, UlJointReceptionPhaseIISwitch, UlJointReception3CellSwitch, and UlHetnetJointReceptionSwitch of UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if CellRadius in a Cell MO is set to a value greater than 100000 m, MumimoPuschPsicSwitch of PsicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • The Ul1R1RJRSwitch&Ul1R2RJRSwitch options must be deselected under the UlJRAntNumCombSw parameter when the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE. The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA1 or SA2.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter is set to SSP5, SSP6, SSP7, or SSP9.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is smaller than 4.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE or when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to LOW_SPEED.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the value of the PreambleFmt parameter is smaller than 4.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the FddTddInd parameter of the Cell is set to CELL_TDD and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA1 or SA2.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the FddTddInd parameter of the Cell is set to CELL_TDD and the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter is set to SSP5, SSP6, SSP7, or SSP9.
    • The AvoidPrachInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • The InterfHighSpeedSW option can be selected under HighSpeedSchOptSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch only when FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The DlVoipBundlingSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter can be selected when the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UlCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, note the following: The HighSpeedFlag parameter can only be set to LOW_SPEED. The CellRadius parameter must be set to a value smaller than or equal to 30000 m. The TxRxMode parameter can only be set to 4T4R or 8T8R.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UlInterEnbCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, note the following: The HighSpeedFlag parameter can only be set to LOW_SPEED. The CellRadius parameter must be set to a value smaller than or equal to 30000 m. The TxRxMode parameter can only be set to 4T4R or 8T8R. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter cannot be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • The SfnLoadBasedAdptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to ON if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the SfnDlSchAttriAdjsSwitch option cannot be selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO if the SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch option is deselected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE in the Cell MO, and the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch(MumimoPuschPsicSwitch) option is selected under the PsicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO can only be set to 2T2R or 4T4R.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), the UlCamcSw(UlCamcSw) option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, and the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE(True), the MultiRruCellMode parameter can only be set to SFN(SFN).
    • The DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with a Cell MO can be selected only if FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • If MultiRruCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • The SimplePairedCompSw(SimplePairedCompSw) option can be selected under the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO only if all of the following conditions are met: The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD). The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE. The UlVmimoSwitch(UlVmimoSwitch) option is selected under the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO. The UlJointReceptionSwitch(UlJointReceptionSwitch) option is selected under the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to ON.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is ON, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO cannot be set to 1T1R.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SA2.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SFN or CELL_COMBINATION.
    • The UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected only when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • If AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, MultiRruCellMode in the associated Cell MO cannot be set to SFN.
    • The AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • If the TM10Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, HighSpeedFlag in the corresponding Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • The TM10Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: (1) FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; (2) MultiRruCellFlag in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; (3) MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO is set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION; (4) SfnDlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE.
    • The TM4TM9AdaptiveSwitch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: (1) FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD; (2) MultiRruCellFlag in the Cell MO is not set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; (3) CrsPortNum in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_4; (4) The TM9Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SfnLoadBasedAdptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to ON if the SfnDlSchSwitch and SfnUlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are both set to JOINT(Joint scheduling).
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD: (1) If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, or the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively. (2) If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 2T2R or 2T4R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to SFN or MPRU_AGGREGATION. In addition, if the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SFN, the SfnDlSchSwitch and SfnUlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be both set to JOINT.
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 4T4R, 2T2R, or 2T4R, the CrsPortNum parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_4, and the HighSpeedFlag parameter must be set to LOW_SPEED.
    • The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected when all of the following conditions are met: (1) MultiRruCellFlag in the associated Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; (2) MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION; (3) The value of CellActiveState is CELL_ACTIVE; (4) TxRxMode is set to 4T4R; (5) The value of NbCellFlag is FALSE.
    • If FddTddInd in an Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the DMIMOJTSwitch option under DMIMOAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected.
    • The DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in an Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and the MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, CELL_COMBINATION, or SFN.
    • The MubfResAdjSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R, respectively.
    • The TurboReceiverSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF(Off) when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • The UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the IntraBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the same MO must be deselected when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • The UlCamcSw and UlInterEnbCamcSw options of the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • The DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF(Off) when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF(Off) when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R.
    • Options UlPAMCSwitch of UlSchExtSwitch and UlCamcSw of CamcSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch cannot be selected simultaneously when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • If the HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, the RachAdjSwitch option of the RachAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 16T16R, respectively, the TurboReceiverSwitch parameter must be set to OFF.
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 16T16R, respectively, the MubfResAdjSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in a Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 16T16R, respectively, and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • The 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if all of the following conditions are met: (1) The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD. (2) The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R. (3) The MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to NORMAL. (4) The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The AsfnFastAttSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected when all the following conditions are met: The FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE, and the TM10Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and BOOLEAN_FALSE respectively, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 4T4R.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, if the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_FALSE. If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 2T2R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, SFN, or CELL_COMBINATION.
    • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED.
    • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when the FddTddInd and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and SA0, respectively.
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO can only be set to CELL_COMBINATION(Cell Combination).
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_COMBINATION(Cell Combination), the SfnUlSchSwitch and SfnDlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to JOINT(Joint scheduling).
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD and SubframeAssignment is not set to SA1 or SA2, then the EicicSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch could not be configured as STATIC.
    • If the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, both DlBandWidth and UlBandWidth in the related Cell MO cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6.
    • The UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of the UlSchExtSwitch parameter can be selected when the DlBandWidth parameter is set to CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Cell MO is selected, the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be deselected.
    • If MtcSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the Cell MO cannot be set to CELL_TDD.
    • If the HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to a value other than LOW_SPEED, AdptPwrMatchingAlgoSw under the CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set off.
    • If NoIntrfSmartIRCEnhSwitch of PuschIrcAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is set on, SmartIrcSwitch of PuschIrcAlgoSwitch must be set on and TxRxMode in Cell must be set to 8T8R.
    • If the EmimoMuMimoSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, CrsPortNum and TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO must be set to CRS_PORT_4 and 4T4R, 4T8R, 8T8R or 32T32R, respectively.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if the EmimoMuMimoSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, DlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO cannot be set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15.
    • If FddTddInd in Cell is set to CELL_TDD, and BFModeUserPwrSwitch of DlPcAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is set on or BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch of DlPcAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is set on, BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch of DlPcAlgoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch must be set off.
    • The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag). If this option is selected, the HighSpeedFlag in the Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag). The SU-MIMO feature does not apply to high-speed mobility scenarios.
    • For an LTE TDD cell, the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell MO is set to SA2(SA2). If this option is selected for an LTE TDD cell, the SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell must be set to SA2(SA2). The SU-MIMO feature takes effect only when the uplink-downlink subframe configuration is SA2.
    • If the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must not be set to 1T1R.
    • For an LTE TDD cell, if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE(True), the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SFN(SFN) or MPRU_AGGREGATION(MPRU_AGGREGATION).
    • If the EmimoCpsSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO must be set to 4T4R, 4T8R, 8T8R or 32T32R, respectively.

    • If FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch or FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, DlCyclicPrefix, HighSpeedFlag, and FddTddInd in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP, LOW_SPEED, and CELL_FDD, respectively.
    • FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch and Tm9JtSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set off when all the following conditions are met: MultiRruCellFlag in the associated Cell is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; MultiRruCellMode in the Cell MO is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN; SfnDlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE; TM9Switch or TM10Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on.

    • If the IntraEnbCspcSw in the CellCspcPara MO is ON, the EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to STATIC or DYNAMIC.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in a CellCspcPara MO and AdptPwrMatchingAlgoSw under the CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both set on.
    • The CellCspcSwitch parameter in a CellCspcPara MO and AdptPwrMatchingAlgoSw under the CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both set on.
    • The Tm9JtSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set off if EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to DYNAMIC, CelleCspcSwitch in the associated CellCspcPara MO is set to ON, or Virtual4T4RSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on.
    • InBandRelayDeNbSwitch of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and CellCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO cannot be both set on.
    • InBandRelayReNbSwitch of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and CellCspcSwitch in the CellCspcPara MO cannot be both set on.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be selected only when the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the CellDss and Cell MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, the frequency supporting the sum of RB quantities specified by HighFreqShareRbNum and LowFreqShareRbNum in the CellDss MO cannot exceed one third of the value of DlBandWidth in the Cell.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch and Cell MOs:
    • If GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO and CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM and NOT_CFG, respectively.
    • If GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch under DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is turned on, DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO and CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd in the Cell MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM and NOT_CFG, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg, CellAlgoExtSwitch, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • When CsiRsSwitch in a CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to FIXED_CFG or ADAPTIVE_CFG, TM9Switch or TM10Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch, FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch or FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch or Tm9JtSwitch of DlCompSwitch, SfnJtSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch, or 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected, or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be selected, or JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH of IntelligentBeamSchSwitch in SectorSplitGroup MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • UlHoppingType in a CellUlschAlgo is not set to HOPPING_OFF, SfnUplinkCompSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch can only be configured to off.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UlCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UlInterEnbCamcSw option is selected under the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO must be set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the UlVoIPLoadBasedSchSwitch option of the UlEnhencedVoipSchSw parameter is selected, the SpsSchSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter must be selected.
    • The sum of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 24 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 49 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N50.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 74 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N75.
    • The sum of the values of UlVoipRsvRbStart in CellUlschAlgo and UlVoipRsvRbNum in CellUlschAlgo cannot be larger than 99 when the UlVoipRbRsvSwitch option of UlSchExtSwitch is selected and DlBandWidth is equal to CELL_BW_N100.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the IntraBaseBandSwitch option of the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO is set to 3dot75K or HYBRID_MODE, the MrcIrcAdptSwitch option of the PuschIrcAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, EuSectorEqmGroup, CellAuxEnbBind, and CellAuxDuBind MOs:
    • Assume that: The FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, the AsfnFastAttSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, and the CellAuxDuBind, and CellAuxEnbBind MOs are not configured. Then: The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when either of the following conditions is met: (a) The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT. (b) The sum of the number of eUCellSectorEqm MO instances and the number of EuSectorEqmGroup MO instances is 2 and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • Assume that: The FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, and the AsfnFastAttSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. Then: The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when either of the following conditions is met: (a) The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT. (b) The sum of the number of eUCellSectorEqm MO instances and the number of EuSectorEqmGroup MO instances is 2 and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm MO:
    • In a Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and there are two eUCellSectorEqm MOs corresponding to the SECTOR MO under which the ANTNUM parameter is set to 8, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option can be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch. If any of the preceding conditions is not met, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option cannot be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch option is selected under the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the ReferenceSignalPwrMargin parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO must be set to 0.
    • The DlAfcSwitch option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the value of the BaseBandEqmId parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is not 255.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the UlInterSiteIcSwitch option of the UplinkIcSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the EicicSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FeicicSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch option of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch option of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the TurboReceiverSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of the Dl256QamAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the AvoidGeranInterfSwitch, AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch, and AvoidUtranInterfSwitch options of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the GLPwrShare parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the UlMultiClusterSwitch option of the UlSchExtSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in an eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw option of the CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompCbfSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO must be set to 0.
  • Relation with the CellDlschAlgo MO:
    • If the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to InterDlCompCbfSwitch or InterDlCompDcsSwitch, the DlschStrategy parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO must be set to DLSCH_PRI_TYPE_EPF.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, all of the following conditions must be met: (1) The value of EmimoIndependentSchThd in the related CellDlschAlgo MO must be greater than or equal to the value of EmimoJointSchThd; (2) The value of EmimoIndependentSchThd must be greater than the value of EmimoJointIndepThdOffset; (3) The value of EmimoJointSchThd must be greater than the value of EmimoJointIndepThdOffset.
    • If the DlSpsMcsIncreaseSwitch option under the CqiAdjAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the DlSpsMcsDecreaseIblerThd parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be set to 10 or larger.
    • If the DediPrioManageOnLowLoadSw option of EnhancedMlbAlgoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the ADAPTIVE_FREQ_PRIORITY_SW option of MultiCarrierCoSchAlgoSw in the associated CellDlschAlgo MO cannot be selected.
    • When the CqiOnlyOuterLoopSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is set to ON, the SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • When the CqiOnlyOuterLoopSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is set to ON, the EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • The EpfEnhancedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be set to ON only if the EpfEnhancedSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the DlMinExpectedBitRate parameter in a CellDlschAlgo MO is set to a value in the range of 0 to 1024, the EpfEnhancedSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellCsiRsParaCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd and MultiRruCellFlag parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and BOOLEAN_TRUE respectively and the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to FIXED_CFG or ADAPTIVE_CFG, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to CELL_COMBINATION, SFN, or MPRU_AGGREGATION.
    • If the SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively, the SfnUlSchSwitch and SfnDlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to JOINT, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO must be set to CSI_RS_PORT_4.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo MO:
    • If PdcchSymNumSwitch in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is not set to OFF, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch parameter cannot be selected.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of EpdcchAlgoSwitch in the related CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected.
    • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the EpdcchFunctionSwitch option of the EpdcchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg MO:
    • If FddSrsCfgMode in the SRSCfg MO is set to ADAPTIVEMODE, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the SrsSubframeCfg parameter is set to SC0.
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the SrsCfgInd parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • If the SrsResOptSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ON(On), the DmimoJTSwitch or InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the SrsCfgInd and FddSrsCfgMode parameters in an SRSCfg MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and DEFAULTMODE, respectively, the PucchMeasOptSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • The WTTxSRSIntrfAvoidanceSw option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is selected, when all the following conditions are met: (a) The DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The IntraDlCompSwitch and IntraDlCompJtSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (c) The UlCamcSw and UlInterEnbCamcSw options of the CamcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the WTTxSRSIntrfAvoidanceSw option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO must not be selected at the same time.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg MO:
    • If PrachConfigIndexCfgInd in the RACHCfg MO is set to NOT_CFG, SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be set to 266DOT7 and the HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the SuperCombCell MO:
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, SuperCombCell MO must have been configured for the cell.
  • Relation with the SfnEdgeRruRelation MO:
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, one or more SfnEdgeRruRelation MOs must have been configured for the cell.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode MO:
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO must be INDEPENDENT.
    • If OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch under RelaySwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO must be set to INDEPENDENT, SHARED_FREQ, or SEPARATED_FREQ.
    • If OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch under RelaySwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ, RanShareModeSwitch in this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAuxDuBind MO:
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, CellAuxDuBind MO cannot be configured for the cell.
  • Relation with the CaGroupCell MO:
    • If SuperCombCellSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the cell could not be configured as CA cell in CaGroupCell.
  • Relation with the Cell, PIMCellGroup, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If CellPIMInterMitigSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON and CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with this CellAlgoSwitch MO is CELL_ACTIVE, LocalCellId in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must have been set in a PIMCellGroup MO. This constraint applies only when the PIM_ADP_CELL_SEL_SWITCH option of the PimSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO:
    • The AVOID_INTERF_SWITCH::AvoidUtranInterfSwitch of AvoidInterfSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be turned on, only when the UlIcicFreqSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is turned off.
    • The InterfHighSpeedSW option can be selected under HighSpeedSchOptSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch only when DlIcicSwitch is set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO, UlIcicFreqSwitch set to OFF in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO, and EicicSwitch set to OFF in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If DlIcicSwitch of the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM, the DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, UlIcicFreqSwitch in the associated ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, DlIcicSwitch in the associated ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM.
    • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when UlIcicFreqSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is turned on.
    • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected when DlIcicSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is turned on.
    • If UtranIdleMlbSwitch of MlbAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, INTER_FREQ_IDLE_MLB_SWITCH of MlbAlgoSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • The DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM if the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF if the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM if the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF if the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the EUCompactRANSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ON.
    • If the ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch option of the HoAllowedSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch is On, the ServiceBasedInterFreqHoSwitch, UtranServiceHoSwitch, or GeranServiceHoSwitch option of the HoAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch cannot be On.
  • Relation with the CellRicAlgo MO:
    • The TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter can be selected when the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter is set to 0.
    • The InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the RemoteInfAdpAvoidSwitch parameter in the associated CellRicAlgo MO is set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellUlschAlgo MO:
    • The InterfHighSpeedSW option can be selected under the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO only when the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to HOPPING_OFF.
    • The VmimoOptResSwitch option can be selected under the VmimoOptAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO only if the SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch option is selected under the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • The NbUlSubcarrierSpacingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO must not be set to 3dot75K if the MULTITONE_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If UlMultiClusterSwitch option of the UlSchExtSwitch parameter is selected, the UlHoppingType parameter in the associated CellUlschAlgo MO must be set to Hopping_OFF.
    • If the SfnUll2SelectiveRcvSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to a value other than MS0.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can only be set to MS0.
    • If the IntvlOfUlSpsWithSkipping parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to a value other than MS0, the CloseLoopSpsSwitch option of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter is selected, the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If FddTddInd in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Cell MO is selected, UlIcicFreqSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SPLIT and the SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • For a Cell with the FddTddInd parameter set to CELL_TDD, the HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, or when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R or 8T8R.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • The DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD(TDD) and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the NoSrsSccBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, either of the following conditions must be met: 1. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • The SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the IntraBandInterfMonitorSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and a SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, then the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the PDSCHCfg MO:
    • If the BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch option is selected under the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the ReferenceSignalPwrMargin parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 0.
    • When the DlNonStandardBwMaxPwrSwitch options of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the CellPowerLimit parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 0.
    • When the GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, the CrsPowerBoostingAmplitude parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to 0 dB.
    • When the GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON, the PdschPowerBoostingSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the LogicalPortSwapSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO is set to SWAP_MODE_1, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellSimuLoad MOs:
    • Consider that MultiRruCellMode in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_COMBINATION or MPRU_AGGREGATION. If the TM9Switch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO is deselected, SimuLoadTransMode in a CellSimuLoad MO under this Cell MO cannot be set to TM9.
  • Relation with the CellPdcchAlgo and Cell MOs:
    • In a TDD cell, if the MSG5Attempt option of HLNetAccSigAttempt in the CellPdcchAlgo MO is selected, HighSpeedFlag in the corresponding Cell MO must be set to LOW_SPEED, the PuschDtxSchOptSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected, and PDCCHAggLvlAdaptStrage in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to STRATEGYBASEDONCAPACITY.
  • Relation with the Cell and NCellSrsMeasPara MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the UL_ICS_SWITCH option under the UlIcSwitch parameter is selected, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be set to ON.
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE and the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the NCellDmrsMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the associated NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CLZeroBufferzone and Cell MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value of ClSharedFreqEndRb1 in the CLZeroBufferzone MO must be less than the maximum number of RBs corresponding to the bandwidth represented by UlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO. The UlBandWidth parameter can be set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, corresponding to the maximum number of RBs 6, 15, 25, 50, 75, or 100, respectively.
    • When the NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value of ClSharedFreqEndRb2 in the CLZeroBufferzone MO must be less than the maximum number of RBs corresponding to the bandwidth represented by UlBandWidth in the associated Cell MO. The UlBandWidth parameter can be set to CELL_BW_N6, CELL_BW_N15, CELL_BW_N25, CELL_BW_N50, CELL_BW_N75, or CELL_BW_N100, corresponding to the maximum number of RBs 6, 15, 25, 50, 75, or 100, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellUSParaCfg MO:
    • The MBSFNSubJTOptSwitch option of UsAlgoSwitch in the CellUSParaCfg MO can be selected only when the SfCtrlAsScell option of UsAlgoSwitch in the CellUSParaCfg MO and the IntraDlCompSwitch option of DlCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected.
    • The JTUserCoCellPowerSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in a CellUSParaCfg MO is mutually exclusive with the BFModeUserPwrSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO. The two options cannot be selected simultaneously.
    • The JTUserCoCellPowerSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in a CellUSParaCfg MO is mutually exclusive with the BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO. The two options cannot be selected simultaneously.
    • If UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld in the CellUSParaCfg MO is set to a value greater than 0, the DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup and Cell MOs:
    • The GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch option of DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50. (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch option of DynSpectrumShareSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
    • The AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected when the cell corresponding to LocalCellId1 or LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup MO meets the following requirements: (1) DdBandwidth is set to BW_5DOT7M; (2) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT3M; (3) DdBandwidth is set to BW_7DOT6M; (4) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT6M; (5) DdBandwidth is set to BW_8DOT9M; (6) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_4M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (7) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_12DOT2M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (8) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_17DOT1M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N15; (9) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_27DOT6M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (10) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_28DOT5M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N50; (11) If DdBandwidth is set to BW_14DOT1M, BW_18DOT7M or BW_19M, DlBandWidth in the Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N25.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • When 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO must be set to CELL_FDD.
    • When Virtual4T4RSwitch or 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, DlCyclicPrefix and UlCyclicPrefix in the associated Cell MO must be set to NORMAL_CP.
    • When New4TCodebookSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the same CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When HighSpeedFlag in a Cell MO is set to HIGH_SPEED, ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED, or EXTRA_HIGH_SPEED, 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the DlBandWidth parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_BW_N6 or CELL_BW_N15, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, respectively, and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in a Cell MO is set to TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION or SFN, respectively, and the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO must be set to 4T4R or 4T8R, or 32T32R and the CrsPortNum parameter must be set to CRS_PORT_4.
    • The CELL_CAPB_BASED_GBR_OPT_SW option of the WTTxAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected only when the BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
  • Relation with the CellMimoParaCfg MO:
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, either of the following conditions must be met: (1) MimoAdaptiveSwitch and FixedMimoMode in the related CellMimoParaCfg MO are set to NO_ADAPTIVE and TM4, respectively; (2) MimoAdaptiveSwitch is set to CL_ADAPTIVE.
    • If FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch or FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, the 8T_TM9_MU_MIMO_SW option of the MimoSwitch in the associated CellMimoParaCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the Cell and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • The TxRxMode parameter in a Cell MO must be set to 2T2R or 4T4R when the following conditions are met: The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively. If the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 4T4R, only one EuSectorEqmGroup MO instance can be configured for this Cell MO.
    • If MultiRruCellFlag in an Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MultiRruCellMode is set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, only one set of cell sector equipment can be configured in each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for the cell when the DMIMOAlgoSwitch option under DMIMOAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SimuloadPmiTemplate MO:
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, PmiType in related SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs must be set to Emimo.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, PmiType in related SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs must be set to Normal.
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, CellSimuloadRank in the related SimuloadPmiTemplate MO must be set to RANK_1 or RANK_2.
  • Relation with the SimuloadPmiTemplate, SimuloadPmiTemplate, and SimuloadPmiTemplate MOs:
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and CellSimuloadRank in a SimuloadPmiTemplate MO is set to RANK_2, CellSimuloadPmiExtend0 and CellSimuloadPmiExtend1 must be set to 11 and 10, respectively.
  • Relation with the PIMCellGroup MO:
    • If PimMitigationMode in a PIMCellGroup MO instance is set to BASED_ON_JOINT_SCH, EmimoCpsSwitch of EmimoSwitch cannot be set on in the CellAlgoSwitch MO instance for either of the cells specified by Cell1Id and Cell2Id in the PIMCellGroup MO instance.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the BfDetectRank1AdjSwitch option of the DlRankDetectSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO must not be selected at the same time.
    • In the Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD and the DlVoIPLoadBasedSchSwitch option of the DlEnhancedVoipSchSw parameter is selected, the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter must be selected.
    • The MaxMimoRankPara parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO can be set to SW_MAX_SM_RANK_8(Rank8) only when the HighSpeedFlag, MultiCellShareMode, MultiRruCellFlag, TxRxMode, and FddTddInd parameters in the Cell MO are set to LOW_SPEED(Low speed cell flag), NORMAL, BOOLEAN_FALSE(False), 8T8R or 32T32R or 64T64R, and CELL_TDD(TDD) respectively and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the IntraDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the InterDlCompDcsSwitch and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the DL_INTRA_JAMC_ALGO_SW option of the DlJointAMCAlgoSw parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the DdCellGroup MO:
    • SfnDlSchSwitch and SfnUlSchSwitch cannot be set to ADAPTIVE in the CellAlgoSwitch instances associated with the Cell instances for the cells specified by LocalCellId1 and LocalCellId2 in a DdCellGroup instance.
  • Relation with the Cell and HighSpdAdaptionPara MOs:
    • The HighspdVectorUpdateSwitch parameter in a HighSpdAdaptionPara MO takes effect only when the option ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed of HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell is not set to LOW_SPEED and the HSCellSleepInterfAvoidSwitch option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CellAuxEnbBind MO:
    • When the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is ON, the CellAuxEnbBind MO cannot be configured for this cell.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellCspcPara MOs:
    • If the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO must be set to OFF.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the BFModeUserPwrSwitch, BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch, and BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch options of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.
    • The CelleCspcSwitch parameter in the CellCspcPara MO can be set to ON only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch are deselected.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, EutranExternalCell, Cell, and SfnEdgeRruRelation MOs:
    • PhyCellId in the EutranExternalCell MO associated with an EutranIntraFreqNCell MO (representing an inter-eNodeB intra-frequency neighboring cell of the local cell) can be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO representing the local cell if both conditions are met: (1) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected in the local cell; (2) All values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO are the same with values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, EnodeBId, and NeighborCellId in at least one SfnEdgeRruRelation MO configured for the local cell. If either condition is not met, PhyCellId in the EutranExternalCell MO cannot be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO.
  • Relation with the EutranIntraFreqNCell, Cell, and SfnEdgeRruRelation MOs:
    • PhyCellId in the Cell MO representing the local cell can be the same as PhyCellId in the Cell MO associated with the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO (representing an intra-eNodeB inter-frequency neighboring cell of the local cell) if both conditions are met: (1) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected in the local cell; (2) All values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, eNodeBId, and CellId in the EutranIntraFreqNCell MO are the same with values of parameters Mcc, Mnc, EnodeBId, and NeighborCellId in at least one SfnEdgeRruRelation MO configured for the local cell. If either condition is not met, values of PhyCellId in the two Cell MOs cannot be the same.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • Among all cells which use a same SECTOREQM, the number of cells which CellActiveState in a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and GLPwrShare in the CellAlgoSwitch is set to ON must be less than 2.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellRelayAlgoPara MOs:
    • If any of the OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, there must be a CellRelayAlgoPara MO instance under the Cell MO. The CellRelayAlgoPara MO instance under the Cell MO can be deleted only if the OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, and InBandRelayReNbSwitch options are deselected.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 1_10, 2_10, or 3_10.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 3_40, 6_40, 9_40, 12_40, 15_40, 18_40, 21_40, 24_40, or 10_10.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 1_10, 2_10, or 3_10.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 3_40, 6_40, 9_40, 12_40, 15_40, 18_40, 21_40, 24_40, or 10_10.
    • If the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the InBandRelayConCnt parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be NON_CON.
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the value of the InBandRelayConCnt parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be NON_CON.
  • Relation with the RelayExtendedQci MO:
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, one and only one RelayExtendedQci MO instance with DefaultBearerInd set to YES must be configured.
  • Relation with the EUEPCSec MO:
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the CiphAlgo parameter in the EUEPCSec MO must not be set to EEA0(NULL).
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and there are two eUCellSectorEqm MOs corresponding to the SECTOR MO under which the ANTNUM parameter is set to 8, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option can be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch. If any of the preceding conditions is not met, the ComCoverUlCompSwitch option cannot be selected for the SfnUplinkCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the Cell MO are 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, and BBP MOs:
