    • In the same eNodeB, the number of Cell MOs related to which the UlJointReceptionSwitch option of UplinkCompSwitch is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot exceed 3 if the following conditions are met: CellActiveState of a Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the eUCellSectorEqm MO of the corresponding Cell MO is bound to a BASEBANDEQM MO, and BRDSPEC of the BBP MO related to the BASEBANDEQM MO is set to WD22LBBPC.
    • The DlAfcSwitch option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to ON for a cell only if the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the TYPE parameter is set to UBBP in the BBP MO that has the same cabinet number, subrack number, and slot number as the BBP specified in the BASEBANDEQM MO.
    • The DlAfcSwitch option of HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected for a cell only if the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the TYPE parameter is set to UBBP or LBBP in the BBP MO that has the same cabinet number, subrack number, and slot number as the BBP specified in the BASEBANDEQM MO.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup, Cell, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • The total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for a Cell MO must be both 4 or 2 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) when the following conditions are met: The Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, CellActiveState, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MPRU_AGGREGATION, CELL_ACTIVE, and 4T4R, respectively.
    • The SectorEqmGrpCombineId parameter in the EuSectorEqmGroup MO must not be 255 and the total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas in the sector equipment configured using the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO for a Cell MO must be both 2 (the TX antennas refer to those whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) when the following conditions are met: The CellActiveState, MultiRruCellFlag, MultiRruCellMode, and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, MPRU_AGGREGATION, and 2T2R, respectively, and the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with a Cell MO is selected and the CellActiveState, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_ACTIVE or CELL_COMBINATION, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and SFN, respectively, the SectorEqmGrpCombineId parameter in each EuSectorEqmGroup MO under the Cell MO must not be 255, and the total numbers of TX antennas and RX antennas configured in all the SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to each EuSectorEqmGroup MO must be both 2.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM, eUCellSectorEqm, and Cell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and ANTCFGMODE in a SECTOREQM MO referenced by a Cell is set to BEAM and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET is set to None, or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of DynAdjVoltSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • If the ANTCFGMODE and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET parameters in the SECTOREQM MO are set to BEAM and NONE respectively, or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • When ANTCFGMODE in a SECTOREQM MO referenced by a Cell MO is set to BEAM and BEAMAZIMUTHOFFSET is set to None, or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the EDlMaxTXPwrSwitch option of DlPcAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the SECTOREQM, Cell, and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is not set to CELL_TDD and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in the SECTOREQM MO referenced by the Cell MO is set to BEAM(BEAM), or the CellBeamMode parameter in eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the ANTCFGMODE parameter in SECTOREQM MO is set to ANTENNAPORT, the PwrDeratSwitch parameter in theCellAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ON(On).
  • Relation with the Cell, EuSectorEqmGroup, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • If the MultiRruCellFlag and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to BOOLEAN_TRUE and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively and the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the cell is selected, no more than one EuSectorEqmGroup MO can be configured for this Cell MO, and no more than one SECTOREQM MO can be configured for the EuSectorEqmGroup MO (if configured).
  • Relation with the Cell and MDU MOs:
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the TurboReceiverSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, the AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of AvoidInterfSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the TYPE parameter in the MDU MO is set to MDUC, other than Tm9JtSwitch, the options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode and LicRatio MOs:
    • If OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch under RelaySwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and ENodeBSharingMode in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or SEPARATED_FREQ, TrafficSharingType in this LicRatio MO must be set to SHARING.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellEicic MOs:
    • In a Cell, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, and SubframeAssignment is set to SA1, and EicicSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is set to STATIC, then the AbsPattern in CellEicic could not have subframe 3 and subframe 8.
  • Relation with the Cell, CellEicic, and CellMBMSCfg MOs:
    • The AbsPattern parameter in the CellEicic MO that is related to the Cell MO can be configured only with subframe 0 and subframe 5 when the following conditions are met: In a Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_TDD, and the SubframeAssignment parameter is set to SA1 or SA2. In the CellMBMSCfg MO that is related to the Cell MO, the MBMSSwitch parameter is set to 1. In the CellAlgoSwitch MO that is related to the Cell MO, the EicicSwitch parameter is set to STATIC.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg and Cell MOs:
    • When NbCellFlag in a Cell MO is set to FALSE, if FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the PucchSwitch option of PucchAlgoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, and PrachFreqOffsetStrategy in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC, PrachFreqOffset in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain.
  • Relation with the NbCellDynPwrShrPara and Cell MOs:
    • When the DlPrbUsageThld parameter in an NbCellDynPwrShrPara MO is set to a non-zero value, the NbCellFlag parameter in the associated Cell MO must be set to TRUE, and the COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CellServiceDiffCfg MO:
    • If the FPIGTPUSwitch or DSCPRemarkSwitch option of ServiceDiffSwitch in the CellServiceDiffCfg MO is selected, neither the VideoRateAdaptSwitch nor DownLoadRateLimitSwitch option of ServiceDiffSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected. If the VideoRateAdaptSwitch or DownLoadRateLimitSwitch option is selected, neither the FPIGTPUSwitch nor DSCPRemarkSwitch option can be selected.
    • If the FPIGTPUSwitch or DSCPRemarkSwitch option of ServiceDiffSwitch in the CellServiceDiffCfg MO is selected, the ScVideoRateAdaptSwitch option of ScVideoOptSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected. If the ScVideoRateAdaptSwitch option is selected, neither FPIGTPUSwitch nor DSCPRemarkSwitch can be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, PrbRachCeConfig, and Prb MOs:
    • If the 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected for a cell, any option (indicating a coverage level) of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in any Prb MO of this cell and the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is FALSE and the EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW option of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO with that coverage level under that Prb MO cannot be both selected, and the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is TRUE and the option in the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO under that Prb cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the CellEdtConfig MO:
    • If the MULTITONE_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the EdtMtSchToneNum parameter in the corresponding CellEdtConfig MO must be set to TONE_1.
  • Relation with the PrbUlSchCeAlgo and PrbRachCeConfig MOs:
    • The MtNprachRangeMsg3InitTone parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO can only be set to ONE_1TONE if the MULTITONE_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the UlInitialTransRptCount parameter in the PrbUlSchCeAlgo MO is set to a value indicating a repetition count greater than or equal to 2, or the PrachRepetitionCount parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO is set to REP_32, REP_64, or REP_128.
  • Relation with the CellEicic MO:
    • In the CellAlgoSwitch MO, if EicicSwitch is set to DYNAMIC, the EicicMeasureEnable in CellEicic must be set to ENABLE.
  • Relation with the CellEicic and Cell MOs:
    • If EicicAdaptiveSwitch in the CellEicic MO is set to ON, FddTddInd in the associated Cell MO and EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to CELL_FDD and DYNAMIC, respectively.
  • Relation with the UlCsAlgoPara MO:
    • If PucchFlexCfgSwitch of PucchAlgoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch of DynSpectrumShareSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch of AvoidInterfSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • If UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in a UlCsAlgoPara MO is set on, SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch of UlSchSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set on.
    • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH and UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH options of the UlCsSw parameter in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO are deselected.
    • UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in a UlCsAlgoPara MO can be set on only if InnerLoopPuschSwitch of UlPcAlgoSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on.
    • If UlVoipRbRsvSwitch of UlSchExtSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH of UlCsSw in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO must be set off.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH and UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH options of the UlCsSw parameter in the associated UlCsAlgoPara MO are deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and SRSCfg MOs:
    • For an LTE TDD cell, if the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO must be set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced). If the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced), the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected. The SU-MIMO feature takes effect only when the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED(Experience Enhanced) or ACCESS_ENHANCED(Access Enhanced).
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, the FddTddInd parameter is set to CELL_FDD; in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter is deselected; in the SRSCfg MO, the FddSrsCfgMode, SrsSubframeCfg, and AnSrsSimuTrans parameters are set to DEFAULTMODE, SC0, and BOOLEAN_TRUE, respectively.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg and Cell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the same MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, if TM9Switch(TM9Switch) under the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to NOT_CFG, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to CSI_RS_PORT_8, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 8T8R or 32T32R or 64T64R.
  • Relation with the CellCspcPara and Cell MOs:
    • FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set off if TxRxMode in the associated Cell MO is set to 1T1R or 1T2R, EicicSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to DYNAMIC, CelleCspcSwitch in the associated CellCspcPara MO is set to ON, or Virtual4T4RSwitch of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on.
  • Relation with the NCellDlRsrpMeasPara MO:
    • If FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch or FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch of DlCompSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set on, DlRsrpAutoNCellMeasSwitch in the associated NCellDlRsrpMeasPara MO must be set to ON.
  • Relation with the Cell, SimuloadPmiTemplate, eUCellSectorEqm, PDSCHCfg, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The SINGLE_CA_PWR_SHARE_SW_FOR_4T option of TxChnPowerCfgSw in the PDSCHCfg MO for a cell must be deselected if any of the following condition is met: TxRxMode in the Cell MO is set to a value other than 4T4R, FddTddInd in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, CrsPortNum in the Cell MO is set to a value other than CRS_PORT_4, CellSimuloadPmi in the SimuloadPmiTemplate MO is set to 4, 5, 6, or 7, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch, Virtual4T4Rswitch, or New4TcodebookSwitch option of EmimoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of Dl256QamAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, or CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to ADVANCED_BEAMFORMING, or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO:
    • When the NoSrsSccBfSwitch option of the NoSrsSccBfAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SccBfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the associated CellBfMimoParaCfg MO must be set to ON.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellRbReserve MOs:
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.
    • When the RbRsvMode parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_DEPLOYMENT(NB_DEPLOYMENT), and the CellActiveState in the Cell MO associated with the CellRbReserve MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.
  • Relation with the TimeAlignmentTimer MO:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the TimingMeasMode parameter in the TimeAlignmentTimer MO is set to INVALID.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg, CellAlgoExtSwitch, SectorSplitGroup, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When TM9Switch and TM10Switch of EnhMIMOSwitch, FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch and Tm9JtSwitch of DlCompSwitch, and SfnJtSwitch of SfnAlgoSwitch, and 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in a CellAlgoSwitch MO, and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO, and JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH of IntelligentBeamSchSwitch in SectorSplitGroup MO are deselected, CsiRsSwitch in the associated CellCsiRsParaCfg MO must be set to NOT_CFG.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo and Cell MOs:
    • Under the same cell, when the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to FALSE and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected, the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the UeCooperationPara MO:
    • When the UlHCodeSwitch option of the UdcAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch is selected, the SpecUeIdentifySwitch option of the SpecUserCooperationSwitch parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO must also be selected.
    • When the VoLTEMoPrefSwitch option of the VoLTESwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the VoltePagingPriority parameter in the UeCooperationPara MO can be set to a value other than OFF.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO:
    • If the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch or FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO and Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ADAPTIVE, the LteInBandInterFreqDeploySw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO cannot be set to ON.
    • If the SPECTRAL_EFF_BASED_GBR_OPT_SW option of the WTTxAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, the BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must also be selected.
    • If the 2D_FSS_SWITCH option of the DlSchEnhSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, the SMART_FSS_SWITCH option of the DlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and Cell MOs:
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 8T8R, or if the CellBeamMode parameter is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode parameter in the associated Cell MO is set to 4T4R, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 and the TxRxMode in the Cell is set to 8T8R, or when the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R under the UlHighOrderCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be turned off.
  • Relation with the CellLteFlexBw MO:
    • The LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO and the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch or GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch option of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected simultaneously.
    • The LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in a CellLteFlexBw MO and the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch or AvoidGeranInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected simultaneously.
    • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected if the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the associated CellLteFlexBw MO is selected.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected if the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the associated CellLteFlexBw MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellEmtcAlgo MO:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the associated CellEmtcAlgo MO is deselected.
    • If the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the PRACH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW, PUSCH_FREQ_HOPPING_SW options of the EmtcFreqHoppingSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId must not be selected.
    • The Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be both selected.
    • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be both selected.
    • If the COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH or SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH option of the CeModeHoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the EMTC_SWITCH options of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO associated through LocalCellId must be selected.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO cannot be both selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud MO:
    • When the DL_PFM_PRF_PDCCH_SWITCH option of the InterfPfmOptSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is selected, the SfnUlSchSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ADAPTIVE(Adaptive scheduling).
    • When the SpsSchSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to OFF.
    • If SpectrumCloudSwitch in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO can be set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the AvoidGeranInterfSwitch, AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch, and AvoidUtranInterfSwitch options of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in a SpectrumCloud MO is set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch options of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to OFF.
    • If the TM9Pusch12Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, the UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_SW option of the SpectrumCloudEnhSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO must be deselected and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in this SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM.
    • If the TM9Pusch12Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is selected, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY.
  • Relation with the NCellSrsMeasPara MO:
    • The SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO must be set to ON(On) if the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The NCellDmrsMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in an NCellSrsMeasPara MO can be selected only when the UlHetnetCompOnUlRsrpSw option of the UplinkCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, CellBfMimoParaCfg, and CellBf MOs:
    • The TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, TxRxMode, and MultiCellShareMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_FALSE, 8T8R, and NORMAL respectively, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter and MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected, the FreqSelSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO is set to FOUR_LAYERS, and the BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
  • Relation with the Cell, SRSCfg, and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD(TDD), the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to EXPERIENCE_ENHANCED or ACCESS_ENHANCED, and the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD; the TddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to ACCESS_ENHANCED; and the MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0.
  • Relation with the HighSpdAdaptionPara and Cell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and HighSpeedFlag parameter in a Cell is not set to LOW_SPEED and an option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and an option of the InterfAvoidMode parameter in the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO associated with this Cell MO is selected, the InterfHighSpeedSW option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellBf MOs:
    • The MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to HIGH_SPEED_OPT_SCHEME, BIG_EVENT_PROTECT_SCHEME, HEAVY_LOAD_SCHEME, or LIGHT_LOAD_SCHEME only when the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD and 64T64R or 32T32R respectively, and the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The BfUePowerLimitSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to ON only if the BFModeUserPwrSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The AdaptMubfStartThld parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to a value ranging from 0 to 100 only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the cell is activated, and the PdschSplitBeamConfigIndex parameter in the CellBf MO is set to CONFIG_INDEX4, then the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, and CellCsiRsParaCfg MOs:
    • The TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, and the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to FIXED_CFG or ADAPTIVE_CFG.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg and CellDlschAlgo MOs:
    • The TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected if the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to CSI_RS_PORT_4 and the CsiRsPeriod parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to ms5, or the UnCfgCsiRsUEDeOrderSchSwitch option of the CsiRsSfSchStrSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected, or the UnCfgCsiRsUESchSwitch option of the CsiRsSfSchStrSwitch parameter in the CellDlschAlgo MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, CspcAlgoPara, CellCspcPara, Cell, and PHICHCfg MOs:
    • The LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW and LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW options of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only when the following conditions are met: The GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO, and the CellCspcSwitch and CelleCspcSwitch parameters in the CellCspcPara MO are set to OFF, the PhichInnerLoopPcSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, and the PhichDuration parameter in the PHICHCfg MO is not set to EXTENDED.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, Cell, and CellUSParaCfg MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO is selected, the MultiCellShareMode and SubframeAssignment parameters in the Cell MO must be set to NORMAL and SA2 respectively, and both the USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, and CspcAlgoPara MOs:
    • The LTE_DYN_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO can be selected only if the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM, the TddCspcMacroMicroSwitch option of the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO is deselected, and both the BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch and BFModeUserPwrSwitch options of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.
  • Relation with the WtcpProxyAlgo MO:
    • The TcpAsSchWeightFactor parameter in the WtcpProxyAlgo MO and the AsForTcpSw option of the ServiceDiffSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be set to non-zero values at the same time.
  • Relation with the CellChPwrCfg MO:
    • The PdcchPowerIncreaseOffset parameter in the CellChPwrCfg MO can be set to a non-zero value only when the BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellBfMimoParaCfg MOs:
    • The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParacfg MO must be set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE when the SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters in the Cell MO are set to CELL_TDD, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION respectively.
    • The TM9_DL_4LAYER_BOOST_SW option of the Tm9Dl4LayerMimoEnhSw parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be selected only when the following conditions are true: In the Cell MO, FddTddInd is set to CELL_TDD, and MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE but MultiRruCellMode is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION; in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO, BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE; in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, Prb, and EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • In the Cell MO, if the NbCellFlag, MultiRruCellFlag, and MultiRruCellMode parameters are set to TRUE, BOOLEAN_TRUE, and MPRU_AGGREGATION, respectively, the TxRxMode parameter can be se to 4T4R only when the following conditions are met: The number of EuPrbSectorEqmGroup MO instances under this Cell MO does not exceed 1; the DeployMode parameter in the Prb MO under this Cell MO is set to IN_BAND; the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated through LteCellId is selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, CellBfMimoParaCfg, CellBf, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • The HEAVYLOADTRANSMODEADAPTBOOSTSW option of the BfMimoAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be selected only when the following conditions are met:
    • The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
    • The BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The SsrdSchOptSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected or the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The WaitPairingLayerThd parameter in the CellBf MO is set to 0.
    • The MultiLayerThdSwitchToTM7 parameter in the CellBf MO is set to 0.
    • The TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, CellMMAlgo, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The MU_PAIRING_MCS_EXACT_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellBf MO:
    • The MovingUeMuBfScheme parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to SPLIT_SDMA_SCHEME only when the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The HighOrderMubfMaxLayer parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to EIGHTEEN_LAYERS, TWENTY_LAYERS, TWENTYTWO_LAYERS, or TWENTYFOUR_LAYERS only if the SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The ChannelRecSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2 or Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in a CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MOs:
    • If the PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LteNrUlSpectrumShrMode parameter in the LteNrSpctShrCellGrp MO cannot be set to STATIC_SHARING.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • When the DlIcicSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to DlIcicSwitch_OFF_ENUM the UlIcicFreqSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, the EicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, and the FeicicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
  • Relation with the CellShortTtiAlgo MO:
    • When the MumimoPuschPsicSwitch option of the PsicSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the InterfRandSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • When the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of the Dl256QamAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
    • The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected only when all of the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch, and InBandRelayDistantDeploySw options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, SpectrumCloud, CellLteFlexBw, and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • When the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to NOT_CFG, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to OFF, the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO is deselected, and the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch and GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch options of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO can be selected.
  • Relation with the CaMgtCfg, CellShortTtiAlgo, and PUCCHCfg MOs:
    • When the PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the CaDl3CCSwitch, CaDl4CCSwitch, and CaDl5CCSwitch options of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO are deselected, and the SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected, the value of the SpucchRbCnt parameter multiplied by 2 must be less than or equal to the value of the CqiRbNum parameter minus 1.
    • The value of the SpucchRbCnt parameter multiplied by 2 must be less than or equal to the value of the CqiRbNum parameter minus 2 when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The CaDl3CCSwitch, CaDl4CCSwitch, or CaDl5CCSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected. (c) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is deselected. (d) The FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is set to a value other than DL_MASSIVE_CA. (e) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected. (f) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The value of the SpucchRbCnt parameter multiplied by 2 must be less than or equal to (the value of the CqiRbNum parameter - the value of the Format3RbNum parameter - 1) when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The CaDl3CCSwitch, CaDl4CCSwitch, or CaDl5CCSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected. (c) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is deselected. (d) The FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is set to a value other than DL_MASSIVE_CA. (e) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected. (f) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The value of the CqiRbNum parameter must be greater than (the value of the SpucchRbCnt parameter x 2 + 2) when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected, or the FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in this MO is set to DL_MASSIVE_CA. (c) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected. (d) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • The value of the CqiRbNum parameter must be greater than (the value of the SpucchRbCnt parameter x 2 + the value of the Format3RbNum parameter + 1) when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected, or the FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in this MO is set to DL_MASSIVE_CA. (c) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is selected. (d) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The value of the CqiRbNum parameter must be greater than the value of the Format3RbNum parameter plus 1 when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected, or the FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in this MO is set to DL_MASSIVE_CA. (c) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is deselected. (d) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The value of the CqiRbNum parameter must be greater than 2 when all the following conditions are met: (a) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (b) The DlMassiveCaSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected, or the FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter in this MO is set to DL_MASSIVE_CA. (c) The SHORT_TTI_SW option of the SttiAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellShortTtiAlgo MO is deselected. (d) The SCCAckResourceCfgSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the ULZeroBufferZone MO:
    • If the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the AvoidGeranInterfSwitch, AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch, AvoidUtranInterfSwitch, and AvoidSpurEmissInterfSwitch options of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch and GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch options of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in a ULZeroBufferZone MO is selected, the SuperCombCellSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CspcAlgoPara MO:
    • The switches indicated by the Sa1Sa2InterfCoordSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the TddCspcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CspcAlgoPara MO cannot be both turned on.
  • Relation with the Cell and GlobalProcSwitch MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, MaksIdxSwitch must be deselected.
    • In a Cell MO, if HighSpeedFlag is set to ULTRA_HIGH_SPEED and HighSpeedR14Switch is selected, RachAdjSwitch must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and SpectrumCloud MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch must be selected.
    • When the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the GLPwrShare parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the PagingInterfRandSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
    • When the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR, or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch and GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch options of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, the DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be set to ON for this cell.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_COORD_SPECTRUM_SHR, none of the options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected for this cell.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellAuxDuBind MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if FddTddInd is set to CELL_FDD, MultiRruCellFlag is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, MultiRruCellMode is set to SFN and CellActiveState is set to CELL_ACTIVE, and the CellAuxDuBind MO is configured for the cell, the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch option of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CaMgtCfg MO:
    • If the CaUserLimitOptSwitch option of the DacqEnhancementSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch is selected, the WbbCaMultiCarrierCoordSw option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO must be deselected.
    • If the PucchMeasOptSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch is selected, the CaDl3CCSwitch, CaDl4CCSwitch and CaDl5CCSwitch options of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO must be deselected and the FddTddCaDlMaxCcNum parameter must not be set to DL_MASSIVE_CA.
    • If the CaRouteNumberExtensionSwitch option of the CellCaAlgoSwitch parameter in the CaMgtCfg MO is selected, the Dl2CCAckResShareSw option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the REMOTE_INTRF_BF_ENH_SW option of the RemoteIntrfDlEnhSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be selected only if the UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO:
    • The PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the REMOTE_INTRF_BF_ENH_SW option of the RemoteIntrfDlEnhSwitch parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO cannot be selected at the same time.
    • If the InterferenceRbRatioThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be set 100 if the InterFreqBackoffSwitch option of the BackoffAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The UL_JOINT_INTRF_POSITION_SW option of the UlJointIntrfManagementSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be selected only when all the following conditions are met: The UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO is set to a value other than 127, the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R option of the UlHighOrderCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
    • If the UlInterSiteIcSwitch or UlIntraSiteIcSwitch option of the UplinkIcSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO for a cell is selected or the UlPAMCSwitch option of the UlSchExtSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected, the UL_JOINT_INTRF_SUPPRESS_SW option of the UlJointIntrfManagementSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO for this cell must be deselected.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig, Cell, Prb, CellRbReserve, and CellDss MOs:
    • When the NbCellFlag parameter in a Cell MO is set to TRUE, the NbRfCarrierStrategy parameter in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig under the same cell is set to SHARE, and under the LTE cell associated by the LteCellId of the Prb, the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch switch on, the RbRsvType parameter in a CellRbReserve MO is set to UPLINK_MODE, and the RbRsvMode parameter in the CellRbReserve MO is set to NB_RESERVED or NB_DEPLOYMENT, then the RbRsvStartIndex and RbRsvEndIndex parameters in the CellRbReserve MO must be set in such a way that the reserved RBs are 2 RBs away from the shared RBs.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode, UlCsAlgoPara, CellEmtcAlgo, and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • The UlSingleSideIntrfImpMitSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO must be set to OFF when one of the following conditions is met: - The ENodeBSharingMode parameter in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is not set to INDEPENDENT. - The UL_COORD_RES_ALLOC_SWITCH or UL_COORD_PC_SWITCH option of the UlCsSw parameter in the UlCsAlgoPara MO is selected. - The EMTC_SWITCH option of the EmtcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellEmtcAlgo MO is selected. - The ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the PrbRachCeConfig MO:
    • If the 100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the PrachSubcarrierNumber parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MOs for all coverage levels and all carriers in the NB-IoT cell can only be set to SC_NUM_12.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitGroup and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • If the JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in a SectorSplitGroup MO is selected, all the following requirements must be met: The SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter must be set to the same value in the CellAlgoSwitch MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitGroup, SectorSplitCell, and Cell MOs:
    • If the CsiRsAdaptBeamCount parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to ADAPTIVE_BEAM_DISABLED and cell is active, the following requirements must be met:
    • The JOINT_SCHEDULING_SWITCH option of the IntelligentBeamSchSwitch parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO must be selected, the 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected, or the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under the Cell MO must be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2 and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO corresponding to the Cell MO is less than or equal to 2 and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the ENodeBAlgoSwitch, Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, SectorSplitCell, SRSCfg, and CellSrsAdaptiveCfg MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, and FddTddInd in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under related Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and related SectorSplitGroup MO includes one or two SectorSplitCell MO instances, and the MM_SRS_ALLOC_OPT_SWITCH option of the UlResManageOptSw parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, all of the following conditions must be met: (a) The SrsCfgInd parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE. (b) The FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to DEFAULTMODE. (c) The SrsPeriodAdaptive parameter in the CellSrsAdaptiveCfg MO is set to ON. (d) The SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SpectrumCloud and Cell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the associated SpectrumCloud MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, then the EicicSwitch, SfnAlgoSwitch, FeicicSwitch parameters must be set to OFF in the associated CellAlgoSwitch MO.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the LowSpeedInterFreqHoSwitch and InterFreqDirectHoSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF and the LowSpeedBlindRedirect option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in this CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD and the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch and UlSchSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When one or more options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch are selected, and the FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO cannot be set to LTE_NR_UPLINK_SPECTRUM_SHR or LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR.
  • Relation with the Cell, SpectrumCloud, and ContigIntraBandCarr MOs:
    • The PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH and PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH options of the NonStandardBwAlgoSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to ON or CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw in the ContigIntraBandCarr is selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, NbCellMultiPrbConfig, and Prb MOs:
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, the COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of PrachCoverageLevelType must be selected in at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the NPRACH_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and STAND_ALONE, respectively.
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to STAND_ALONE in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, the COVERAGE_LEVEL_0 option of PrachCoverageLevelType MOs must be selected in at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The value of CellActiveState in the Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the NPRACH_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND, respectively.
  • Relation with the NbCellMultiPrbConfig, Prb, and PrbSchConfig MOs:
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to STAND_ALONE in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, PagingWeight must be set to a value other than W0 in the PrbSchConfig MO under at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the PAGING_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND, respectively.
    • If AnchorCarrierFlag is set to FALSE and DeployMode is set to GUARD_BAND or IN_BAND in some Prb MOs under a Cell MO for an NB-IoT cell meeting specific conditions, PagingWeight must be set to a value other than W0 in the PrbSchConfig MO under at least one of these Prb MOs. The specific conditions are as follows: The MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of NbCellAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under the Cell MO is selected, the PAGING_PRB_SELECTION_SW option of MultiCarrierOptSwitch in the NbCellMultiPrbConfig MO under the Cell MO is selected, and AnchorCarrierFlag and DeployMode in a Prb MO under the Cell MO are set to TRUE and STAND_ALONE, respectively.
  • Relation with the Cell, SpectrumCloud, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO under this Cell MO is set to LTE_NR_SPECTRUM_SHR, and the SymbolShutdownSwitch option of the PowerSaveSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the MBSFNShutDownSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the HighSpdAdaptionPara MO:
    • The DL_AFC_OPT_SW option of the HighSpeedOptSwitch is selected, the DlAfcSwitch option of the HighSpeedSchOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell and MPT MOs:
    • If TYPE in the MPT MO is set to LMPT, MtcSwitch in the associated CellAlgoSwitch cannot be set to ON.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, SectorEqm, CellCsiRsParaCfg, and Cell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the same MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, and the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_COMBINATION or SFN, if TM9Switch(TM9Switch) under the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter is selected in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the CsiRsSwitch parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is not set to NOT_CFG, and the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to 8, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to 8T8R, and the number of TX antennas (whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER) specified by the SECTOREQMANTENNA parameter in the SECTOREQM MO corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm MO must not be 4.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, SECTOREQM, and RRU MOs:
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • When the MultiRruCellMode parameter in a Cell MO is not set to MPRU_AGGREGATION and the TP parameter is set to BRANCH(BRANCH) in the RRU MO mapping the SECTOREQM MO indicated by the SectorEqmId parameter specified in the eUCellSectorEqm MO in the same Cell MO, the DMIMOJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the EuSectorEqmGroup and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • The SfnJtSwitch option of the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the total numbers of both TX and RX antennas specified in the SECTOREQM MO associated with the EuSectorEqmGroup MO in the Cell MO are 2. The preceding TX antennas refer to the TX antennas whose TX Antenna Master/Slave Mode is set to MASTER.
    • If the Cell MO is associated with at least one EuSectorEqmGroup MO which is configured with more than 16 SECTOREQM MOs, the TDOA_SWITCH option of the RruUeMapSwitch and LbsSwitch parameters in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState in a Cell is set to CELL_ACTIVE and the CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm of the Cell is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of the SectorSplitCell belonging to the SectorSplitGroup to which the Cell belongs equals 1 or 2, UlCompForVideoSwitch under the UplinkCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be turned off.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, Cell, and SECTOREQM MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the value of the CellActiveState parameter is CELL_ACTIVE, if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in this Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to SFN or CELL_COMBINATION, and the Virtual4T4Rswitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the SectorEqmCombineGrpId parameter in each eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell must not be set to 255, and the number of TX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2. In addition, if the Cell::FddTddInd parameter in this Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the number of RX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2 or 4; if the Cell::FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the number of RX antennas of the sector equipment configured in each SECTOREQM MO corresponding to each eUCellSectorEqm MO must be 2.
  • Relation with the CellMBMSCfg MO:
    • The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MBMSSwitch option of the MBMSSwitch parameter in the CellMBMSCfg MO cannot be both selected.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO and the MBMSSwitch option of the MBMSSwitch parameter in the CellMBMSCfg MO cannot be both selected.
  • Relation with the ClkDetect MO:
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only if the ClkAsyncDetectSwitch parameter in the associated ClkDetect MO is set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellRicAlgo and CellRicAlgo MOs:
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 2. The DuctDLSubfrmShutoffSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to OFF. 3. The DUCT_DL_SUBFRM_SHUTOFF_OPT_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is deselected. 4. The DUCT_DL_SF_SCHED_SHUTOFF_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is deselected.
    • The InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The UlInterfSuppressionSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 2. The DuctDLSubfrmShutoffSwitch parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to OFF. 3. The DUCT_DL_SUBFRM_SHUTOFF_OPT_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is deselected. 4. The DUCT_DL_SF_SCHED_SHUTOFF_SW option of the DuctDLSubfrmShutoffOptSw parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the CellRelayAlgoPara MO:
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the value of the DlRelaySubframeRatio parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO must be 1_10, 2_10, 3_10, 4_10, 5_10, 6_10, 7_10, 8_10, 9_10, or 10_10.
    • If the InBandRelayConCnt parameter in the CellRelayAlgoPara MO is set to NON_CON, the InBandRelayReNbSwitch and InBandRelayDeNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be both selected.
  • Relation with the eUCellSectorEqm, Cell, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is less than or equal to two, the New4TcodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • When the CellActiveState parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2, and the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances in the SectorSplitGroup MO associated with the Cell MO is less than or equal to two, the Pusch32Switchoption of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the CellMMAlgo, Cell, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The PRECISE_PAIRING_OPT_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only when the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured and the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, NCellSrsMeasPara, PUSCHCfg, CellUlIcAlgo, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • When the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and no SectorSplitCell MO is configured for the Cell MO, the MASSIVE_MIMO_SRS_IC_SW option of the SrsIcSwitch parameter in the CellUlIcAlgo MO can be selected only if the SrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the SrsAutoNCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO is set to ON, the NCellSrsTimeMeasSwitch option of the NCellMeasSwitch parameter in the NCellSrsMeasPara MO is selected, the GroupHoppingEnabled in the PUSCHCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, and the SeqHoppingEnabled parameter in the PUSCHCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
  • Relation with the SRSCfg, RACHCfg, Cell, SpectrumCloud, CellLteFlexBw, and ContigIntraBandCarr MOs:
    • The PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH option of the NonStandardBwAlgoSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected when all the following five conditions are true: The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected; the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to AUTOMATIC or FULLY_AUTOMATIC; the AnSrsSimuTrans parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE; the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected; the CustomizedBandWidthCfgInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CFG, and the CompactBwCtrlIntrfMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value other than NORMAL, or the SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is set to GL_SPECTRUM_CONCURRENCY, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR or UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, or the LTE_FB_CFG_SWITCH option of the LteFlexBwSwitch parameter in the CellLteFlexBw MO is selected, or the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO is selected.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg, Cell, PUCCHCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, SpectrumCloud, and ULZeroBufferZone MOs:
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N15, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N25, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N50, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected. (b) The NbCellFlag, FddTddInd, and UlBandWidth parameters in the Cell MO are set to FALSE, CELL_FDD, and CELL_BW_N75, respectively. (c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
    • The PrachFreqOffset parameter in the RACHCfg MO must be properly set so that the PRACH RBs and PUCCH RBs do not overlap in the frequency domain under the following conditions: (a) The SpectrumCloudSwitch parameter in the SpectrumCloud MO is not set to UL_SPECTRUM_SHARING, UL_SPECTRUM_SHR_PH2_WITH_GSM, or DC_HSDPA_BASED_UL_SPECTRUM_SHR, the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO is deselected, or the UMTS_LTE_ZERO_BUFFER_ZONE_SW option of the ZeroBufZoneSwitch parameter in the ULZEROBUFFERZONE MO and the PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are selected.
