(c) The PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. (d) The PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is not set to FULLY_AUTOMATIC.
  • Relation with the EnodebMpruEs and Cell MOs:
    • The TDOA_SWITCH option of the LbsSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected for cells with the DlEarfcn parameter in the Cell MO set to the same value as the MpruDormancyDlEarfcn parameter in the EnodebMpruEs MO.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and Cell MOs:
    • If the PUSCH_INTRF_RSRP_HIGH_THLD_SW option of the HeavyLoadPrfmOptSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is selected, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO must be set to MPRU_AGGREGATION, and the PuschRsrpHighThdSwitch option of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the CellUlschAlgo and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The SpaDomainPrefVmimoStrtThld parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO can be set to a value other than 255 only if both the SPACE_DOMAIN_MEAS_SW option of the MeasOptAlgoExtSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO and the UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO corresponding to the Cell MO are selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The SPACE_DOMAIN_MEAS_SW option of the MeasOptAlgoExtSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO can be selected only if all of the following conditions are met:
    • 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD.
    • 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R.
    • 3. The SubframeAssignment parameter in the Cell MO is set to SA2.
    • 4. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
    • 5. The BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • 6. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured.
    • 7. The MultiCellShareMode parameter in the Cell MO is not set to SPLIT.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, CellMMAlgo, SRSCfg, and CellBf MOs:
    • The MaxBfRankPara parameter in the CellBf MO can be set to FOUR_LAYER_BF only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_2. 5. The MM_ANT_SEL_ENH_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is selected. 6. The TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected. 7. The SrsResExpansionSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to OFF.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, and SectorSplitGroup MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, and the SectorSplitCell MO corresponding to the Cell MO exists, then the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then the BfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, the MuBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, and the TM9Switch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch must be deselected.
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then the ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch option of the UlSuMimoAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell and eUCellSectorEqm MOs:
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, and more than six eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured, then the DmimoJTSwitch option of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must not be selected.
    • In a Cell MO, if the CellActiveState parameter is set to CELL_ACTIVE, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE, the MultiRruCellMode parameter is set to CELL_COMBINATION, the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to LOW_SPEED, and more than six eUCellSectorEqm MO instances are configured, then neither the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter nor the SfnUlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be set to ADAPTIVE.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the Dl256QamAccessorySwitch option of Dl256QamAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw option of CellDlCoverEnhanceSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the EDlMaxTXPwrSwitch option of DlPcAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the 8T4PortTM9EnhanceSwitch option of EnhMIMOSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then PwrDeratSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF.
    • If the value of CellActiveState in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, FddTddInd and TxRxMode in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R option of UlHighOrderCompSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected and the UlVmimoSwitch option of UlSchSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must also be deselected.
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the FDDHomNetDlCompSwitch and FDDHetNetDlCompSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the ScAppParaCfg MO:
    • The AsForTcpSw option of the ServiceDiffSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when the AppIdentType parameter in at least one ScAppParaCfg MO is set to TCPPORT.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode, LicRatio, and UlCsAlgoPara MOs:
    • The UlCraSupportMocnSwitch parameter in the UlCsAlgoPara MO can be set to ON only if the following conditions are true: The ENodeBSharingMode parameter in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or HYBRID_SHARED, the RanShareModeSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, and the TrafficSharingType parameter in the LicRatio MO is set to SHARING.
  • Relation with the Prb MO:
    • The MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected for a cell only if the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in the Prb MO is set to FALSE for this cell.
    • If the WorkMode parameter in a Prb MO is set to DL_ONLY and the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO associated with the Prb MO through the LocalCellId parameter is selected, all options of the PrachCoverageLevelType parameter in the Prb MO must be deselected and option EDT_RANDOM_ACCESS_SW of the PrachAlgoSw parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig under this Prb must be deselected.
  • Relation with the PrbSchConfig and Prb MOs:
    • If the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the AnchorCarrierFlag parameter in a Prb MO is set to FALSE and the PagingWeight parameter in the PrbSchConfig MO under this Prb MO is not set to W0, the PrbBlockPriority parameter in this Prb MO must be set to UNBLOCKED.
  • Relation with the Prb and PrbSchConfig MOs:
    • When the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO referenced by a Prb MO through the LocalCellId parameter is selected: If the WorkMode parameter in the Prb MO is set to UL_ONLY, the PrbIndexOfDlPdcchCarrier parameter in the PrbSchConfig MO under the Prb MO cannot be set to 65535 or the value of the PrbId parameter in any Prb MO in which the WorkMode parameter is set to UL_ONLY.
    • When the MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH option of the NbCellAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO referenced by a Prb MO through the LocalCellId parameter is selected: If the WorkMode parameter in the Prb MO is set to UL_DL, the PrbIndexOfDlPdcchCarrier parameter in the PrbSchConfig MO under the Prb MO cannot be set to the value of the PrbId parameter in any Prb MO in which the WorkMode parameter is set to UL_ONLY.
  • Relation with the RACHCfg, PrbRachCeConfig, and Prb MOs:
    • The actual duration corresponding to the value of the PrachTransmissionPeriod parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO for a PRB at a certain coverage level must not be less than the PRACH transmission duration for this coverage level of the PRB. The details are as follows: If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 66DOT7(66.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 5.6. If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 266DOT7(266.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 6.4.
    • The actual duration corresponding to the value of the PrachTransmissionPeriod parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO for a PRB at a certain coverage level must not be less than the PRACH transmission duration for this coverage level of the PRB. The details are as follows: If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 66DOT7(66.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 5.6. If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 266DOT7(266.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 6.4.
    • The actual duration corresponding to the value of the PrachTransmissionPeriod parameter in the PrbRachCeConfig MO for a PRB at a certain coverage level must not be less than the PRACH transmission duration for this coverage level of the PRB. The details are as follows: If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 66DOT7(66.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 5.6. If the NbCyclicPrefixLength parameter in the RACHCfg MO referenced by the PRB MO through the LocalCellId parameter is set to 266DOT7(266.7us), the PRACH transmission duration for the corresponding coverage level is the result of the value of the PrachRepetitionCount parameter for the anchor carrier multiplied by 6.4.
  • Relation with the RlcPdcpParaGroup MO:
    • For all the RlcPdcpParaGroup MOs with the RlcMode parameter set to RlcMode_AM, if the RlcParaAdaptSwitch parameter is set to OFF, the DlRLCStateReportSchDelaySw option of the DlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the CellOp MO:
    • When the OpPrbGroupshareSwitch option of OpResourceGroupShareSwitch is selected, all OpNonGbrResourceRatio in same operator group must be set to the same value.
    • If the RanShareModeSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO is set to ON and the RbSharingRatioWithOtherOp parameter in a CellOp MO under this Cell MO is set to a value other than 255, the RbSharingRatioWithOtherOp parameter in this CellOp MO cannot be set to a value greater than the value of the OpUlRbUsedRatio or OpDlRbUsedRatio parameter in this CellOp MO.
    • The WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH and WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH options of the SpecUserAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under a Cell MO can be selected only when the RbSharingRatioWithOtherOp parameter is set to 255 in all the CellOp MOs under this Cell MO.
    • In a CellOp MO, the value of the OpMaxAvailableRbRatio parameter must be greater than or equal to the values of both the OpUlRbUsedRatio and OpDlRbUsedRatio parameters if the RanShareModeSwitch parameter in the corresponding CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON.
    • The RbSharingRatioWithOtherOp parameter in the CellOp MOs under a Cell MO can be set to a value other than 255 only when the RanShareModeSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO is set to ON.
  • Relation with the CellQciPara and CellRacThd MOs:
    • When the McpttCongCtrlSwitch option of the McpttSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the sum of the HandoverAdmissionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-65 services and the value of the NewIronServiceOffset parameter in the CellRacThd MO must be less than or equal to 100.
    • When the McpttCongCtrlSwitch option of the McpttSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the sum of the HandoverAdmissionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-66 services and the value of the NewIronServiceOffset parameter in the CellRacThd MO must be less than or equal to 100.
    • When the McpttCongCtrlSwitch option of the McpttSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the sum of the CongestionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-1 services and the value of the CongRelOffset parameter in the CellRacThd MO must be less than the value of the HandoverAdmissionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-66 services.
    • When the McpttCongCtrlSwitch option of the McpttSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the sum of the CongestionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-65 services and the value of the CongRelOffset parameter in the CellRacThd MO must be less than the value of the HandoverAdmissionThreshold parameter in the CellQciPara MO for QCI-65 services.
  • Relation with the CellFrameOffset MO:
    • If the InBandRelayReNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the CellFrameOffset MO must be configured and the FrameOffsetMode parameter in this MO must be set to CustomFrameOffset.
  • Relation with the CellWttxParaCfg MO:
    • when the IptvGuaranteeUserCount in the CellWttxParaCfg is not equal to 0, the VideoRateAdaptSwitch option of the ServiceDiffSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch must be selected.

  • Relation with the CellRbReserve MO:

    • When the PATTERN_ADJ_SWITCH option of the SectorSplitSwitch parameter in a SectorSplitGroup MO is selected, and the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to CELL_FDD, all the following requirements must be met:
    • (a) The SrsCfgInd parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to BOOLEAN_TRUE.
    • (b) The FddSrsCfgMode parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to Default Mode.
    • (c) The SrsSubframeCfg parameter in the SRSCfg MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to the same value.
    • (d) The SrsSubframeRecfSwitch option of the SrsAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MOs corresponding to all SectorSplitCell MOs under this SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to the same value.
  • Relation with the Cell and CellMMAlgo MOs:
    • The MM_ACCESS_CONTROL_OPT_SWITCH option of the MMAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO can be selected only if the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, and the AcBarAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC or the EABAlgoSwitch option of the MTCCongControlSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected.
    • The DL_CLUSTER_PAIRING_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO under a Cell MO can be selected only when all the following conditions are met: The TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R, the MuBfSwitch and SplitSDMASwitch options of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO are selected and deselected, respectively, and the PRECISE_PAIRING_OPT_SW option of the PreciseMubfOptSwitch parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO is deselected.
    • The MubfTbsForecastPairLyrThld parameter in the CellMMAlgo MO under a Cell MO is not equal to zero only when all the following conditions are met: (1) The FddTddInd in the Cell is set to CELL_TDD; (2) The TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R; (3) The MuBfSwitch options of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO is selected.
  • Relation with the CellAlgoExtSwitch and CellEnhCoverage MOs:

  • Relation with the ContigIntraBandCarr MO:
    • If the GLZeroBufferZoneSwitch or GLDynSpectrumShareSwitch option of the DynSpectrumShareSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO must be deselected.
    • If the AvoidUtranInterfSwitch or AvoidCDMAInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the CONTIG_INTRA_BAND_CARR_SW option of the ContigIntraBandCarrSw parameter in the ContigIntraBandCarr MO must be deselected.

  • Relation with the SymbolPwrSaving and Cell MOs:

    • If the TrigBndlSchDlAvgPrbThld parameter in the SymbolPwrSaving MO is not set to 0 for a cell and the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD for the cell, the DmimoJTSwitch and InterCellDmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected for the cell.

(b) The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_1, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be 3, and the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected. (c) The BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO must be set to MODE_2, the number of SectorSplitCell MO instances under the SectorSplitGroup MO must be 4 or 5, and the New4TCodebookSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.

The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 32T32R or 64T64R. The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, CellUSParaCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R, 32T32R, or 8T8R, the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then: 1. The UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be set to 0; 2. The USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be deselected; 3. The PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to NONE; 4. The PdcchSdmaWeightType parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO cannot be set to POWER_SHARING_AMONG_3_BEAMS, POWER_SHARING_AMONG_4_BEAMS, or POWER_SHARING_AMONG_5_BEAMS; 5. The BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected; 6. The MimoUePaAdaptSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF; 7. The EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell and Cell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, and the SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is configured, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO is set to NOT_CFG, then in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the following options must be deselected: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH option of the EnhChnCalSwitch parameter, IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter, TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, AntSelEnhanceBfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch and DmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter, UlCamcSw option of the CamcSwitch parameter, and UlVmimoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter, and HighOrderVMIMOSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter.
  • Relation with the Cell, SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, ENodeBAlgoSwitch, CellUSParaCfg, CellPdcchAlgo, and PDSCHCfg MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 8T8R, there is a SectorSplitCell MO that corresponds to this Cell MO, the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in the SectorSplitGroup MO that includes this SectorSplitCell MO is set to MODE_3, the SsrdDynDlPwrSharingSwitch option of the SSRDAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is selected, then the following parameter setting requirements must be met: The UsUeDlAdaptPwrBoostThld parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be set to 0, the USPwrSharePaSwitch option of the UsAlgoExSwitch parameter in the CellUSParaCfg MO must be deselected, the PdcchSparePowerAllocStrage parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be set to NONE, the MASSIVE_MIMO_PDCCH_SDMA_SW option of the SplitBeamPdcchSdmaSw parameter in the CellPdcchAlgo MO must be deselected, the BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch option of the DlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected, the MimoUePaAdaptSw parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF, and the EmfPowerLimitSwitch parameter in the PDSCHCfg MO must be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the CellPcAlgo MO:
    • The OUTER_LOOP_PUCCH_PC_OPT_SW option of the PcAlgoOptSwitch parameter in the CellPcAlgo MO can be selected only when the OuterLoopPucchSwitch and GroupPCForPucchSwitch options of the UlPcAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are both selected.
  • Relation with the Cell, SRSCfg, CellRicAlgo, CellBf, and SectorSplitCell MOs:
    • The SrsResExpansionSwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO can be set to SRS_RES_EXPANSION_MODE only when all of the following conditions are met: 1. The FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD. 2. The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 64T64R or 32T32R. 3. The SectorSplitCell MO associated with the Cell MO is not configured. 4. The WTTxSRSIntrfAvoidanceSw option of the SrsCfgPolicySwitch parameter in the SRSCfg MO is set to OFF. 5. The SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 6. The MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter in the CellRicAlgo MO is set to 0. 7. The PucchFlexCfgSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. 8. The LARGE_DELAY_SPREAD_UE_IDENT_SW option of the UeIdentificationSwitch parameter in the CellBf MO is selected.
  • Relation with the SectorSplitCell, SectorSplitGroup, and Cell MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the TxRxMode parameter in this Cell MO is set to 8T8R, the cell specified by this Cell MO is added in a SectorSplitCell MO under a SectorSplitGroup MO, and the BeamDeploymentMode parameter in this SectorSplitGroup MO is set to MODE_3, then in the CellAlgoSwitch MO, the following options must be deselected: QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH option of the EnhChnCalSwitch parameter, IntraDlCompSwitch, InterDlCompDcsSwitch, and InterDlCompCbfSwitch options of the DlCompSwitch parameter, TurboBfSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter, AntSelEnhanceBfSwitch option of the BfAlgoSwitch parameter, InterCellDmimoJTSwitch and DmimoJTSwitch options of the DMIMOAlgoSwitch parameter, and UlCamcSw option of the CamcSwitch parameter.
  • Relation with the CellIntelAmcConfig MO:
    • When the DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in the CellIntelAmcConfig MO is selected, the EicicSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO must be set to OFF.
    • The DL_INTEL_AMC_SELECTION_SW option of the IntelligentAmcSwitch parameter in a CellIntelAmcConfig MO can be selected only if CqiAdjAlgoSwitch option of the CqiAdjAlgoSwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON.
  • Relation with the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO:
    • If the CsiRsPortNum parameter in the CellCsiRsParaCfg MO is set to CSI_RS_PORT_8 and the TM9HybridPrecodingSwitch option of the EnhMIMOSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the TM9SplitSdmaSwitch option of the MuBfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, CellAlgoExtSwitch, and UlInterfSuppressCfg MOs:
    • The UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg must be set to 127 when the FddTddInd parameter in the Cell MO is set to CELL_TDD, the NbCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to TRUE, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected, the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO is set to ON, or the UlCyclicPrefix parameter in the Cell MO is set to EXTENDED_CP.
  • Relation with the UlInterfSuppressCfg, eUCellSectorEqm, and CellUlschAlgo MOs:
    • The UlIntrfDetectionTrigThld parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO can be set to a value other than 127 when all of the following conditions are met: The TxRxMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to 1T2R, 2T2R, 2T4R, or 4T4R, the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO is set to NORMAL, the HighSpeedFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to LOW_SPEED, the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE, the InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch, and OutOfBandRelaySwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO are deselected, the UlHoppingType parameter in the CellUlschAlgo MO is set to HOPPING_OFF, and the CellRadius parameter in the Cell MO is set to a value smaller than or equal to 15000 m.
  • Relation with the CellDlpcPdsch, CellAlgoExtSwitch, and CellShortTtiAlgo MOs:
    • When the sub-switch Virtual4T4RSwitch of EmimoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is configured to be on, or the sub-switch EmimoMuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is configured to be on, or the sub-switch New4TCodebookSwitch of EmimoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is configured to be on, or the sub-switch 4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch of EmimoSwitch in CellAlgoSwitch is configured as open, or GLPwrShare in CellAlgoSwitch is configured as ON, or the sub-switch Tm9FourLayerPairSwitch of WttxMuMimoSwitch in CellAlgoExtSwitch is configured as on, or when the sub-switch SHORT_TTI_SW of SttiAlgoSwitch in CellShortTtiAlgo is configured to be on, RemainingPwrUseStrategy in CellDlpcPdsch must be configured as OFF.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, and EuSectorEqmGroup MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, if the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then:The InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, InBandRelayReNbSwitch and InBandRelayDistantDeploySw option of RelaySwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected.
  • Relation with the CellDynPowerSharing MO:
    • If the LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO under a Cell MO is selected, the Virtual4T4RSwitch option of the EmimoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
    • If the LTE_EXTREME_POWER_SHARING_SW option of the DynamicPowerSharingSwitch parameter in the CellDynPowerSharing MO under a Cell MO is selected, the OutOfBandRelaySwitch and InBandRelayDeNbSwitch options of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be deselected.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, BASEBANDEQM, BBP, EuSectorEqmGroup, CellLcsCfg, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • For a Cell MO in which the values of CellActiveState, FddTddInd, and NbCellFlag are CELL_ACTIVE, CELL_FDD, and FALSE, respectively, if the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs corresponding to this Cell MO are bound to BASEBANDEQM MOs, and WM is set to SPEC_MODE1 and only the LTE_FDD option or only the LTE_FDD and NBIOT options of BBWS are selected in the BBP MOs corresponding to these BASEBANDEQM MOs, then: (1) UlCyclicPrefix and DlCyclicPrefix in the Cell MO cannot be set to EXTENDED_CP. (2) CellRadius cannot be set to a value larger than 100000. (3) MultiRruCellMode cannot be set to TWO_RRU_COMBINATION. (4) The SuperCombCellSwitch option of SfnAlgoSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected. (5) The NB_OTDOA_SWITCH option of CellLcsSwitch in the CellLcsCfg MO and the OtdoaSwitch option of LcsSwitch in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO cannot be selected. (6) TxRxMode in the Cell MO cannot be set to a value larger than 4T4R. (7) CellBeamMode in the eUCellSectorEqm MOs cannot be set to MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph1 or MASSIVE_MIMO_Ph2. (8) ANTCFGMODE in the SECTOREQM MOs corresponding to the eUCellSectorEqm or EuSectorEqmGroup MOs cannot be set to BEAM.
  • Relation with the Cell, eUCellSectorEqm, CellLowPower, and CellRfShutdown MOs:
    • If the value of the CellActiveState parameter in a Cell MO is CELL_ACTIVE, the FddTddInd and TxRxMode parameters in this Cell MO are set to CELL_FDD and 8T8R, respectively, and the CellBeamMode parameter in the eUCellSectorEqm MO under this Cell MO is set to NORMAL, then the DynAdjVoltSwitch option of the DynAdjVoltSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO under this Cell MO must be set deselected, the LowPwrSwitch parameter in the CellLowPower MO under this Cell MO must be set to OFF, and the RfShutdownSwitch parameter in the CellRfShutdown MO under this Cell MO must also be set to OFF.
  • Relation with the UlInterfSuppressCfg and CellAlgoExtSwitch MOs:
    • The UL_JOINT_INTRF_SUPPRESS_SW option of the UlJointIntrfManagementSw parameter in the UlInterfSuppressCfg MO for a cell can be selected only when the UlCoverageEnhancementSw parameter in the CellAlgoExtSwitch MO for the cell is set to OFF and the UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R option of the UlHighOrderCompSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO for the cell is deselected.
  • Relation with the CellBfMimoParaCfg and Cell MOs:
    • The BfMimoAdapWithTm4Switch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be set to ON only if all of the following conditions are met: The CrsPortNum parameter in the Cell MO is set to CRS_PORT_4. The BfSwitch check box is selected under the BfAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO. The BfMimoAdaptiveSwitch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO is set to MIMO_BF_ADAPTIVE.
    • The BfMimoAdapWithTm4Switch parameter in the CellBfMimoParaCfg MO can be set to ON only if the MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_FALSE or all of the following conditions are met: The MultiRruCellFlag parameter in the Cell MO is set to BOOLEAN_TRUE. The MultiRruCellMode parameter in the Cell MO is set to SFN. The SfnDlSchSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to JOINT, or the SfnDlSchSwitch parameter is set to ADAPTIVE and the SfnDl2LoadLevelAdptSwitch check box is selected under the SfnAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO.
  • Relation with the CellSrsAdaptiveCfg MO:
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if SuperCombCellSwitch under SfnAlgoSwitch is set to On, SrsPeriodAdaptive in CellSrsAdaptiveCfg must be configured to Off.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if SuperCombCellSwitch under SfnAlgoSwitch is set to ON, UserSrsPeriod in CellSrsAdaptiveCfg can not be configured to ms5.
    • In a CellAlgoSwitch, if SuperCombCellSwitch under SfnAlgoSwitch is set to ON, UserSrsPeriod in CellSrsAdaptiveCfg can not be configured to ms10.
  • Relation with the Cell, NsaDcMgmtConfig, RACHCfg, and ENodeBAlgoSwitch MOs:
    • If the FddTddInd parameter in a Cell MO is set to CELL_FDD, the TDM_SWITCH option of the NsaDcAlgoSwitch parameter in the NsaDcMgmtConfig MO associated with the cell can be selected when either of the following conditions is met: 1. The PrachConfigIndexCfgInd parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to CFG and the PrachConfigIndex parameter in the same MO is set to 0, 3, 6, 9, 13, 14, 16, 19, 22, 25, 29, 32, 35, 38, 41, 45, 48, 51, 54, or 57. 2. The PrachConfigIndexCfgInd parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to NOT_CFG, the PrachTimeStagSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to OFF, and the RachAdjSwitch option of the RachAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is deselected.
  • Relation with the EUEPCInfo MO:
    • The OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO can be selected only when there is an EUEPCInfo MO instance.
  • Relation with the EuSubscriber and Cell MOs:
    • If the SubscriberType parameter in the EuSubscriber MO is set to RRN, the Cell MO referenced by the CellId parameter in the EuSubscriber MO must be configured, and the OutOfBandRelaySwitch or InBandRelayDeNbSwitch option of the RelaySwitch parameter in at least one Cell MO must be selected.
  • Relation with the ENodeBSharingMode and LicenseSharingPolicy MOs:
    • If the OutOfBandRelaySwitch, InBandRelayDeNbSwitch, or InBandRelayReNbSwitch of the RelaySwitch parameter in a CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected and the ENodeBSharingMode parameter in the ENodeBSharingMode MO is set to SHARED_FREQ or SEPARATED_FREQ, the LicenseSharingPolicy MO must not be configured.
  • Relation with the eNodeBFlowCtrlPara MO:
    • If McpttMsgCntSwitch in the eNodeBFlowCtrlPara MO takes effect, the number of cells with the McpttCongCtrlSwitch option of McpttSwitch in the CellAlgoSwitch MO selected must be greater than 0.

Related MML Commands

Table 1 describes the MML commands related to this MO.

Table 1 Related MML commands

MML Command

Function
DSP CELLULCAMCCLUSTER

Use this command to query the CAMC coordinated cell group of a cell. This command applies only to LTE TDD.
DSP CELLULICSSTATUS

Use this command to query the status of the UL ICS algorithm for a specified cell. This command applies only to LTE TDD.
LST CELLALGOSWITCH

Use this command to list the settings of cell-level algorithm switches.
MOD CELLALGOSWITCH

Use this command to modify the settings of cell-level algorithm switches.

Related Parameters

Table 2 and Table 3 describes the parameters related to this MO.

Table 2 Configuration parameters

Parameter

Description
Local cell ID

Indicates the local ID of the cell. It uniquely identifies a cell within an eNodeB.
VoLTE Redirect Switch

Controls whether VoLTE services support redirection.

If this parameter is set to ON, VoLTE services support redirection and QCI-1 UEs can be redirected. If this parameter is set to OFF, redirection is not supported for QCI-1 UEs. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
RACH algorithm switch

RACHAdjSwitch: Indicates whether to enable random access channel (RACH) resource adjustment, which adaptively adjusts the RACH resources in the cell based on the access type and the number of access attempts. If this option is deselected, RACH resource adjustment is disabled. When the eMTC function takes effect, adaptive adjustment of PRACH period in the RACH resource adjustment algorithm no longer takes effect on LTE and eMTC UEs. Adjustment on the numbers of random preambles and dedicated preambles applies only to LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HoRaSwitch: Indicates whether the random access mode is applied during handovers. If this option is selected, the eNodeB instructs UEs to use the non-contention-based random access mode during handovers. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB instructs UEs to use the contention-based random access mode during handovers. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UnsyncRaSwitch: Indicates whether the random access mode is applied when UEs are out of synchronization in the uplink. If this option is selected, the eNodeB instructs UEs to use the non-contention-based random access mode upon downlink data arrivals in the case of out-of-synchronization. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB instructs UEs to use the contention-based random access mode upon downlink data arrivals in the case of out-of-synchronization. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MaksIdxSwitch: Indicates whether to reuse dedicated preambles among UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB enables reuse of dedicated preambles among UEs based on the MaskIndex parameter. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB allocates a dedicated preamble to only one UE at a time. When the eMTC function takes effect, dedicated preamble reuse does not take effect on LTE and eMTC UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

BackOffSwitch: Indicates whether to enable backoff control. If this option is selected, backoff control is enabled. If this option is deselected, backoff control is disabled.

UeRaInforInqSwitch: Indicates whether the query of UE random access information is enabled. If this option is selected, the query of UE random access information is enabled, and the eNodeB sends the UEInformationRequest message to the UE complying with 3GPP Release 9 and later and requires the UE to report random access information. The eNodeB uses the reported random access information for RACH resource adjustment. If this option is deselected, the query of UE random access information is disabled. The eNodeB uses the self-collected random access information for RACH resource adjustment. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PRACHFalseAlarmDetSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PRACH false alarm detection. If this option is selected and the PrachFalseAlarmDetRadThd or RachThdBoostRatio parameter is not set to 0, PRACH false alarm detection is enabled. If the eNodeB considers the initial contention-based preamble sent by a UE as a PRACH false alarm, the eNodeB does not send the UE a Random Access Response message, nor records the preamble on the performance counter L.RA.GrpA.Att or L.RA.GrpB.Att if the preamble belongs to group A or B, respectively. If this option is selected and the PrachFalseAlarmDetRadThd and RachThdBoostRatio parameters are set to 0, PRACH false alarm detection is disabled. If this option is deselected, PRACH false alarm detection is disabled. When the eMTC function takes effect, PRACH false alarm detection takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

RachPwrAdjSwitch: Indicates whether to enable power adjustment for the RACH. If both this option and UeRaInforInqSwitch are selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the parameters related to power control for the RACH based on the number of random access preamble transmissions, probability of successful random accesses, and target probability of successful random accesses. If either this option or UeRaInforInqSwitch is deselected, power adjustment for the RACH is disabled. When the eMTC function takes effect, power adjustment for the RACH takes effect only on LTE UEs.

ForbidAcByRadiusSwitch: Indicates whether the eNodeB restricts contention-based random access of UEs based on the distance from UEs to the eNodeB. If this option is selected, only UEs whose distance to the eNodeB is less than the cell radius are allowed to use the contention-based random access mode in the cell. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not restrict contention-based random access of UEs based on the distance. When the eMTC function takes effect, this function takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PrachFreqAdjSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PRACH frequency-domain position adaptation. If the PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter is set to AUTOMATIC, this option is selected and minimum PUCCH resources are configured, the PRACH frequency-domain position is adaptively adjusted based on the PUCCH resource usage. When the eMTC function takes effect, this function takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PRACHFalseAlaDetRetxOptSwitch: Indicates whether to allow the access from abnormal UEs that retransmit preambles at an interval of 40 ms when PRACH false alarm detection is enabled. When this option is selected, the eNodeB can identify preambles retransmitted at an interval of 40 ms and allow the access from UEs transmitting these preambles. When this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot identify preambles retransmitted at an interval of 40 ms. When the eMTC function takes effect, this function takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PrachFreqAbsAdjSwitch: Indicates whether to select a frequency domain position adjacent to the PUCCH for the PRACH, regardless of the amount of allocated PUCCH resources. If this option is selected, the PucchSwitch option of the PucchAlgoSwitch parameter is selected, and the PrachFreqOffsetStrategy parameter in the RACHCfg MO is set to AUTOMATIC, the eNodeB selects a frequency domain position adjacent to the PUCCH for the PRACH. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ExceedRadiusRaDetectionSw: Indicates whether the eNodeB checks the access of UEs whose distance to the eNodeB is greater than the cell radius. The eNodeB checks the access of UEs whose distance to the eNodeB is greater than the cell radius only when this option is selected. This option does not apply to cells established on the LBBPc. When the eMTC function takes effect, this option takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NonContRaOptSwitch: Indicates the access (non-contention) function for UEs whose distance to the eNodeB is greater than the cell radius. If this option is selected, non-contention-based random access optimization is performed on the UEs whose distance to the eNodeB is greater than the cell radius. This ensures the access of such UEs. If this option is deselected, non-contention-based random access optimization is disabled. This option does not apply to cells established on the LBBPc. When the eMTC function takes effect, this option takes effect only on LTE UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
SoundingRS algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable SRS-related algorithms for a cell.

SrsSubframeRecfSwitch: Indicates whether to enable cell-specific SRS subframe reconfiguration. If this option is selected, the algorithm dynamically adjusts the SRS subframe configuration based on the usage of cell resources. If this option is deselected, the algorithm uses the initial configuration and does not perform dynamic adjustment. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SrsTimeMeasSwitch: Indicates whether to enable SRS time-domain measurement. If this option is selected, SRS time-domain measurement will be performed in the cell. If this option is deselected, SRS time-domain measurement is not supported and SRS frequency-domain measurement will be performed instead. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
PUCCH algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable PUCCH algorithms in a cell. This parameter includes the following options:

PucchSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PUCCH resource adjustment.

If this option is selected, the eNodeB initiates PUCCH resource adjustment when PUCCH resources are insufficient or excessive.

If this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot adjust PUCCH resources.

PucchFlexCfgSwitch: Indicates whether to enable flexible PUCCH configuration. For LTE FDD, this option does not take effect if an LBBPc is used or the cell bandwidth is 1.4 MHz or 3 MHz. If flexible PUCCH configuration is enabled, frequency-domain uplink ICIC and uplink frequency hopping scheduling do not take effect. For LTE TDD, this option does not take effect when one of the following conditions is met: (1) An LBBPc is used. (2) The cell bandwidth is 1.4 MHz, 3 MHz, or 5 MHz. (3) The TddSrsCfgMode parameter is set to ACCESS_FIRST or EXPERIENCE_FIRST. (4) The MuteUpPTSSymNum parameter is set to a non-zero value or the TddAvoidUpPTSInterfSwitch option of the AvoidInterfSwitch parameter is selected. (5) The SubframeAssignment parameter is set to a value other than SA0, SA1, or SA2, or the SpecialSubframePatterns parameter is set to a value ranging from SSP0 to SSP4 (for a normal CP) or a value ranging from SSP0 to SSP3 (for an extended CP). If flexible PUCCH configuration is enabled, frequency-domain uplink ICIC, uplink ICS, and uplink frequency hopping scheduling do not take effect. If this option is selected, an equal number of RBs (specified by the PucchExtendedRBNum parameter) are separately added to both ends of the uplink band allocated for the PUCCH. The total number of extension PUCCH RBs equals to the PucchExtendedRBNum parameter value multiplied by two, and the extension RBs can be used for PUSCH scheduling. If this option is deselected, flexible PUCCH configuration is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

Dl2CCAckResShareSw: Indicates whether to enable PUCCH ACK channel resource sharing for downlink 2CC CA. The eNodeB divides the ACK code channel resources on the PCC for downlink 2CC CA into four groups. If this option is deselected, CA UEs scheduled on each SCC associated with this PCC are allowed to use the ACK channel resources in only one group. If this option is selected, CA UEs scheduled on each SCC associated with this PCC can use the ACK channel resources in any group.

SCCAckResourceCfgSW: Indicates whether to enable SCC ACK resource configuration. If this option is selected, the eNodeB controls the maximum number of RBs that can be used by PUCCH format 3 and the maximum number of code channels that can be used by SCC ACKs in 2CC CA scenarios according to the settings of the Format3RBNum and Max2CCAckChNum parameters in the PUCCHCfg MO, respectively. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB controls the maximum number of RBs that can be used by PUCCH format 3 and the maximum number of code channels that can be used by SCC ACKs in 2CC CA scenarios.

EmtcAckModeSw: Indicates the HARQ feedback mode of eMTC UEs. If this option is deselected, eMTC UEs always send HARQ feedback in bundling mode. If this option is selected, eMTC UEs always send HARQ feedback in multiplexing mode when the csi-NumRepetitionCE, mPDCCH-NumRepetition, and pucch-NumRepetitionCE-format1 IEs are all set to 1. If any of the IEs is not set to 1, eMTC UEs send HARQ feedback in bundling mode. This option is deselected by default. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

PucchMeasOptSwitch: Indicates whether the baseband processing unit (BBP) uses the PUCCH CQI RSRP measurement algorithm. The optimized PUCCH CQI measurement algorithm is used only after this option is selected. Specifications are affected when the measurement algorithm changes, which also changes the PUCCH resource allocation algorithm. If this option is deselected, the original PUCCH CQI measurement algorithm and PUCCH resource allocation algorithm are used. This option does not apply if LBBPc is used. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
Congestion Control Switch for MTC UE

Indicates whether to enable congestion control algorithms for machine type communication (MTC), enhanced Machine Type Communication (eMTC), and NB-IoT UEs.

EABAlgoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable extended access barring (EAB). When EAB is enabled, an eNodeB implements access class control for EAB-applicable UEs if the E-UTRAN or core network is overloaded. EAB is enabled only if this option is selected. When MtcSwitch is set to ON, you are advised to select the EABAlgoSwitch option to reduce the impact caused by a large number of MTC UEs.

ExtendedwaittimeSwitch: Indicates whether the eNodeB includes the extendedWaitTime parameter into the message sent to UEs when the UEs' RRC connection setup requests are rejected or their RRC connections are released due to core network overload. If this option is selected, the eNodeB includes the extendedWaitTime parameter into the message sent to UEs in either of the following conditions: (a) After receiving an overload message from the MME, the eNodeB rejects the RRC connection setup requests with the cause value DelayTolerant or releases the RRC connections with the cause value DelayTolerant. (b) The eNodeB releases the RRC connections when the NB-IoT cell is congested. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not include the extendedWaitTime parameter into the message sent to UEs in the preceding situations. In an NB-IoT cell, this option also specifies whether to enable the overload control function for RRC connection request messages and connection resume messages.
AQM algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable the Active Queue Management (AQM) algorithm.

If this switch is on, the AQM function is enabled to prevent or control congestion. The length of the data transmission queue is maintained at a relatively small value to help achieve an appropriate trade-off between throughput and latency.

If this switch is off, the AQM function is disabled. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
CQI Adjust Algorithm Switch

CqiAdjAlgoSwitch: Indicates whether to allow the eNodeB to adjust the UE-reported CQI based on the initial block error rate (IBLER). If this option is selected, the CQI adjustment algorithm is enabled. In this case, the eNodeB adjusts the UE-reported CQI based on the IBLER. If this option is deselected, the CQI adjustment algorithm is disabled. In this case, the eNodeB does not adjust the UE-reported CQI based on the IBLER. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

StepVarySwitch: Indicates whether to enable variable-step-based adjustment. If this option is selected, variable-step-based adjustment is enabled to accelerate the convergence of IBLER. In this case, rapid adjustment at large steps is applied if there is a large difference between the measured IBLER and target IBLER; fine-tuning at small steps is applied if the measured IBLER is close to the target IBLER. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is performed at a fixed step. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DlVarIBLERtargetSwitch: Indicates whether to enable downlink target IBLER adaptation. If this option is selected, the downlink target IBLER is adaptively adjusted based on the TBS to improve spectral efficiency. If this option is deselected, the target IBLER is determined based on the setting of the DlEnVarIblerTargetSwitch option of this parameter. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

TddBundlingCqiAdjOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable optimized CQI adjustment in ACK bundling mode. If this option is selected, optimized CQI adjustment is enabled. If this option is deselected, optimized CQI adjustment is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TddMultiplexingCqiAdjOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable optimized CQI adjustment in ACK multiplexing mode. If this option is selected, optimized CQI adjustment is enabled. If this option is deselected, optimized CQI adjustment is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

AdaptiveStepVarySwitch: Indicates whether to enable adaptive step change in CQI adjustment. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD. If this option is selected, CQI adjustment is performed at a large step when significant changes in the signal quality are detected, accelerating IBLER convergence. If this option is deselected, CQI adjustment is performed at a fixed step.

DlCqiAdjDeltaOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize CQI adjustment value calculation. If this option is deselected, the CQI adjustment value is calculated using the following formula: CQI adjustment value = (Number of ACKs for initial transmissions x CQI adjustment value per ACK + Number of NACKs for initial transmissions x CQI adjustment value per NACK)/(Number of NACKs for initial transmissions + Number of ACKs for initial transmissions). If this option is selected, the CQI adjustment value is calculated using the following formula: CQI adjustment value = Number of ACKs for initial transmissions x CQI adjustment value per ACK + Number of NACKs for initial transmissions x CQI adjustment value per NACK. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

DlEnVarIblerTargetSwitch: Indicates whether to enable enhanced downlink target IBLER adaptation. If this option is selected, the downlink target IBLER is adaptively adjusted based on CQI fluctuation and TBS. If this option is deselected, the downlink target IBLER policy is controlled by the DlVarIBLERtargetSwitch option of this parameter. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DlRetxTbsIndexAdjOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize TBS index adjustment in retransmissions. If this option is selected, TBS index adjustment in retransmissions is optimized. The TBS index for the scheduling of the last two retransmissions is lowered, and the TBS index for the scheduling of other retransmissions is determined based on the CQI adjustment result. If this option is deselected, TBS index adjustment in retransmissions is not optimized. The TBS index for the scheduling of any retransmission is determined based on the CQI adjustment result. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CfiConvertOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize CFI conversion. If this option is selected, new CFI calculation tables for normal and special subframes are used to ensure more accurate MCS selection in normal and special subframes and facilitate IBLER convergence to the target value. If this option is deselected, the original CFI calculation tables for normal and special subframes are used. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

BundlingOptThdSwitch: Indicates whether to adjust the MCS index threshold for enabling the optimized CQI adjustment algorithm in TDD bundling mode. If this option is selected, the lower MCS index threshold for enabling the optimized CQI adjustment algorithm in TDD bundling mode is changed from MCS index 6 to MCS index 0. If this option is deselected, the lower MCS index threshold for enabling the optimized CQI adjustment algorithm in TDD bundling mode is MCS index 6. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

DlSpsMcsIncreaseSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MCS index increase for semi-persistent scheduling (SPS). If this option is selected, MCS index increase is enabled for SPS, reducing the number of RBs for SPS and improving cell performance when RB resources are limited. However, the VoLTE MOS value may decrease. If this option is deselected, MCS index increase is disabled for SPS, increasing the VoLTE MOS value. However, more RBs may be consumed for SPS. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CqiAdjTimeEffSwitch: Indicates whether to enable validity evaluation and adjustment for CQI adjustment values, and whether to trigger large-step-based CQI adjustment when a long time has elapsed before a UE begins to perform services based on the setting of the AdaptiveStepVarySwitch option of this parameter. If this option is selected, the eNodeB evaluates the validity of all CQI adjustment values in a unified manner and adjusts these adjustment values based on the evaluation results. If this option and the AdaptiveStepVarySwitch option are selected, large-step-based CQI adjustment is triggered when a long time has elapsed before a UE begins to perform services. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not evaluate the validity of CQI adjustment values or adjust these values in a unified manner, and large-step-based CQI adjustment is not triggered when a long time has elapsed before a UE begins to perform services. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

RptCqiFltInitSwitch: Indicates whether to adjust the initial filtered value of the reported CQI. If this option is selected, the initial filtered value of the reported CQI is determined based on the CQI value reported by the UE, and the filtering coefficient for the reported CQI in the first several reporting periods is adjusted. If this option is deselected, the initial filtered value of CQIs reported by all UEs is fixed to 4. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UeSigInitCqiModSwitch: Indicates the time when the reported initial CQI takes effect. If this option is selected, the reported initial CQI takes effect when the ACK to Msg4 HARQ retransmission is received or when Msg5 is received. If this option is deselected, the reported CQI immediately takes effect. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

CqiFastCompstSwitch: Indicates whether the eNodeB performs fast CQI compensation for the following UEs: UEs handed over to cells whose values of PCI modulo 3 conflict with source cells' values of PCI modulo 3, beamforming UEs in 8T8R cells, and UEs reporting aperiodic CQIs during network access in busy hours. If this option is selected, the eNodeB identifies these UEs and quickly compensates for their CQIs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not identify these UEs for CQI compensation. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

CfiConvertWiDrsSwitch: Indicates whether to consider the DMRS overhead when converting the control format indicator (CFI) for beamforming UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB considers the DMRS overhead when converting the CFI of beamforming UEs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not consider the DMRS overhead when converting the CFI of beamforming UEs in open-loop TM7, TM8, or TM9. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

IblerCqiOffsetSwitch: Indicates whether to perform CQI compensation based on the target IBLER. The eNodeB performs CQI compensation based on the target IBLER only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

CqiFltOptSwitch: Indicates whether to optimize CQI filtering. If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines whether to discard a UE-reported CQI based on this reported CQI and the previous HARQ retransmission BLER, does not filter CQIs again in case of TM change, and triggers both large step increase and large step decrease during step adaptation. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB directly uses UE-reported CQIs, filters CQIs again in case of TM change, and triggers only large step increase during step adaptation. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

PreciseMcsAdaptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable precise MCS adaptation. If this option is selected, the eNodeB selects a TBS with a higher index for the first downlink HARQ retransmission. In addition, when the initial CQI adjustment value is relatively conservative, the eNodeB increases and compensates for the CQI adjustment value. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform the preceding operations. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PilotOffCqiAdjOptSwitch: Indicates CQI adaptation optimization for CRS transmission disabling. If this option is selected, for UEs regarded as large-packet UEs when the BreathingPilotSwitch or EmimoCpsSwitch option is selected, the CQI adjustment is performed at a large step in case of significant signal quality changes. If this option is deselected, the CQI adjustment is performed at a fixed step for the preceding UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

CqiFltSpectralEffOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CQI-filtering-based spectral efficiency optimization. If this option is selected, the eNodeB optimizes the allocation of spectral efficiency on codewords based on the imbalance between the CQIs reported by the UE and the filtered values. If this option is deselected, the function does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Power Saving Switch for MTC UE

Indicates whether to enable power-saving functions for MTC and eMTC UEs and UEs with the RRC connection setup cause value delayTolerant. MTCPowerSavSwitch: This option controls the following two functions: core-network-assisted adjustment of the UE inactivity timer and UEs' reporting of assistance information. In core-network-assisted adjustment of the UE inactivity timer, an eNodeB dynamically adjusts the UE inactivity timer based on the Expected UE Behaviour parameter delivered from the core network. In UEs' reporting of assistance information, UEs report assistance information based on whether the eNodeB provides power preference indications. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Dynamic adjust voltage algorithm switch

Indicates the switch used to enable or disable dynamic voltage adjustment. If this switch is on, the power amplifier (PA) bias voltage is adjusted based on the load of the eNodeB, which reduces power consumption of the PA and improves energy efficiency of the eNodeB. Dynamic voltage adjustment applies only to cells with a bandwidth higher than 5 MHz. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
RAC algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable admission and load control algorithms.

DlSwitch: Indicates whether to enable satisfaction-rate-based downlink admission control. If this option is selected, satisfaction-rate-based downlink admission control is enabled. If this option is deselected, satisfaction-rate-based downlink admission control is disabled. During the calculation of the satisfaction rate of services with different QCIs, the satisfaction estimation method dedicated to VoIP services is implemented on services with QCI 1. If a service with QCI 1 is not a VoIP service, the satisfaction rate calculated using this method is lower than the actual value, which affects the admission of GBR services. Therefore, if all services with QCI 1 are not VoIP services, it is recommended that this option be deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlSwitch: Indicates whether to enable satisfaction-rate-based uplink admission control. If this option is selected, satisfaction-rate-based uplink admission control is enabled. If this option is deselected, satisfaction-rate-based uplink admission control is disabled. During the calculation of the satisfaction rate of services with different QCIs, the satisfaction estimation method dedicated to VoIP services is implemented on services with QCI 1. If a service with QCI 1 is not a VoIP service, the satisfaction rate calculated using this method is lower than the actual value, which affects the admission of GBR services. Therefore, if all services with QCI 1 are not VoIP services, it is recommended that this option be deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DlLdcSwitch: Indicates whether to perform downlink load control for a cell. If this option is selected, the eNodeB checks for congestion in the downlink for the cell. If the cell is congested in the downlink, the eNodeB performs load control for the cell. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not check for congestion in the downlink for the cell and downlink congestion of the cell, if any, cannot be relieved. During the calculation of the satisfaction rate of services with different QCIs, the satisfaction estimation method dedicated to VoIP services is implemented on services with QCI 1. If a service with QCI 1 is not a VoIP service, the satisfaction rate calculated using this method is lower than the actual value, which affects the cell load control. Therefore, if all services with QCI 1 are not VoIP services, it is recommended that this option be deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlLdcSwitch: Indicates whether to perform uplink load control for the cell. If this option is selected, the eNodeB checks for congestion in the uplink for the cell. If the cell is congested in the uplink, the eNodeB performs load control for the cell. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not check for congestion in the uplink for the cell and uplink congestion of the cell, if any, cannot be relieved. During the calculation of the satisfaction rate of services with different QCIs, the satisfaction estimation method dedicated to VoIP services is implemented on services with QCI 1. If a service with QCI 1 is not a VoIP service, the satisfaction rate calculated using this method is lower than the actual value, which affects the cell load control. Therefore, if all services with QCI 1 are not VoIP services, it is recommended that this option be deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PreemptionSwitch: Indicates whether to enable resource preemption. If this option is selected, the eNodeB allows high-priority services to preempt resources for admission when admission of such services fails. If this option is deselected, only emergency calls can be admitted when resources are insufficient. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PttAcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable RB usage-based admission control for PTT services. If this option is selected, RB usage-based admission control is enabled for PTT services. If this option is deselected, RB usage-based admission control is disabled for PTT services. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoltePrefAdmissionSwitch: Indicates whether to enable preferential access of VoLTE services. This option takes effect only when the EutranVoipSupportSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is set to ON(On). If this option is selected, VoLTE services can be preferentially admitted to the network. If this option is deselected, VoLTE services cannot be preferentially admitted to the network. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoltePreemptionSwitch: Indicates whether to allow VoLTE UEs to preempt resources of other UEs to ensure preferential access of VoLTE services. This option takes effect only when the VoltePrefAdmissionSwitch option of this parameter is selected. If this option is selected, the preemption is enabled. If this option is deselected, the preemption is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UeExpBasedCongCtrlSw: If this option is selected, the eNodeB reduces the number of uplink and downlink scheduling times for UEs with poor signal quality when the cell is congested. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not take the preceding action when the cell is congested. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EmcAdmitCongSwitch: Indicates the admission and congestion control policies for emergency calls. If this option is selected, emergency call services can be admitted based on the QoS satisfaction rate when the MCPTT QoS Management and Congestion Control for Public Safety features take effect. In addition, services with a higher ARP priority than that of emergency call services can preempt resources for emergency call services, which means that emergency call services may be released during congestion control. If this option is deselected, emergency call services can be admitted directly and cannot be preempted or released. This option takes effect only when both the MCPTT QoS Management and Congestion Control for Public Safety features take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DlDelayBasedSatisStatSw: Indicates whether to enable downlink delay-based satisfaction rate statistics mode. If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses this mode for downlink delay-sensitive services with QCIs of 3 and 75 and the statistics about these services are collected together. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB inherits the mode that is used in earlier versions for services with QCI of 3. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Load balancing algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable mobility load balancing (MLB) algorithms. This parameter provides the following options: InterFreqMlbSwitch, UtranMlbSwitch, GeranMlbSwitch, UtranIdleMlbSwitch, MlbLoadInfoSwitch, InterFreqBlindMlbSwitch, InterFreqIdleMlbSwitch, VoIPOffloadSwitch, PTTOffloadSwitch, and MbfcsSwitch. If one option is selected, the corresponding algorithm is enabled to balance the load between the serving cell and neighboring cells of the specified category.

InterFreqMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-frequency MLB.

UtranMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MLB to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable MLB to GERAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranIdleMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable "MLB to UTRAN by transferring RRC_REL UEs." This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MlbLoadInfoSwitch: Indicates whether the load of an inter-RAT cell is considered when MLB is performed between an E-UTRAN cell and an inter-RAT cell. This inter-RAT cell must be a UTRAN cell in the current version. If both the MlbLoadInfoSwitch and UtranMlbSwitch options are selected, a UTRAN cell whose load status has been obtained and is normal or medium can be a candidate cell for MLB to UTRAN. If both the MlbLoadInfoSwitch and UtranIdleMlbSwitch options are selected, a UTRAN cell whose load status has been obtained and is normal can be a candidate cell for MLB to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterFreqBlindMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-frequency MLB without load information exchange. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterFreqIdleMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-frequency MLB for UEs in idle mode. If this option is selected, this type of MLB takes effect only if at least one inter-frequency MLB mode is selected for RRC_REL UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoIPOffloadSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the VoIP offload function. If this option is selected, satisfaction rate assessment is performed on VoIP services. If the satisfaction rate of VoIP services is lower than the preset threshold, inter-frequency or inter-RAT offload of VoIP services is triggered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PTTOffloadSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the PTT voice service offload function. If this option is selected, satisfaction rate assessment is performed on PTT voice services (with QCIs 65 and 66 and enhanced extended QCIs). If the satisfaction rate of PTT voice services is lower than the preset threshold, inter-frequency offload of PTT voice services is triggered. If this option is deselected, PTT voice service offload is disabled. The enabling of the PTT offload function is dependent on the VoIPOffloadSwitch option. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MbfcsSwitch: Indicates whether to enable intra-RAT multi-band fast carrier selection. Intra-RAT multi-band fast carrier selection is enabled only when the option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MmLoadSteeringSw: Indicates whether to enable offload between massive MIMO cells and inter-frequency non-massive-MIMO cells. This function is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterVendorMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-vendor MLB. This function is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CovBasedMlbEnhSw: Indicates whether to enable enhanced coverage-based MLB. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. The setting of this option takes effect only if the InterVendorMlbSwitch option of the MlbAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranMlbUeSelectOptSw: Indicates whether to enable preferential selection of cell-edge UEs for load transfer in user-number-based MLB to UTRAN. If this option is selected, the eNodeB preferentially selects cell-edge UEs for load transfer when user-number-based MLB to UTRAN is triggered. If this option is deselected, this function does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
TCP Control Switch

Indicates whether to enable TCP-related functions.

TstpRemovalSwitch: Indicates whether to enable TCP timestamp removal. TCP timestamp removal is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Turbo Receiver Switch

Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to turbo receiver.

The value OFF indicates that the turbo receiver algorithm and enhanced turbo receiver algorithm are disabled.

The value ON indicates that the turbo receiver algorithm is enabled for UEs with an uplink MCS index lower than 9. This value applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

The value ENHANCED_TURBO_RECEIVER_ON indicates that the enhanced turbo receiver algorithm is enabled. In this case, the enhanced turbo receiver algorithm is enabled for UEs with an uplink MCS index lower than 9 and for non-64QAM UEs with an uplink MCS index higher than or equal to 9. This value applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

The value VOICE_TTIB_TURBO_RECEIVER_ON indicates that the PUSCH TTI bundling turbo receiver decoding algorithm is enabled and preferentially applies to TTI bundling UEs running uplink voice services. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
Network-Assisted ICS Switch

Indicates whether to enable network assisted interference cancellation and suppression (NAICS). When NAICS is enabled, the eNodeB delivers intra-frequency neighboring cell information to UEs to help the UEs mitigate the CRS and PDSCH interference from intra-frequency neighboring cells. For details about NAICS, see 3GPP TS 36.331 and 36.423. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Downlink power control algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable power control for the PDSCH, PDCCH, and PHICH.

PhichInnerLoopPcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PHICH inner-loop power control. If this option is deselected, only the initial transmit power for the PHICH is set. If this option is selected, the eNodeB controls the physical channel transmit power for the convergence of the receive SINR to the target SINR. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PdcchPcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PDCCH power control. If this option is deselected, power is allocated evenly on the PDCCH. If this option is selected, the PDCCH power is adjusted dynamically. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EDlMaxTXPwrSwitch: If this option is deselected, the maximum transmit power of the cell is determined by the RS power and the scaling factor indexes Pa and Pb. If this option is selected, the maximum transmit power of the cell can be increased to improve the cell RB usage. This option has no impact on 10 MHz, 15 MHz and 20 MHz LTE TDD cells. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

BFModeUserPwrSwitch: Indicates whether to enable power optimization for beamforming UEs. If this option is deselected, the original power allocation scheme is adopted for beamforming UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses the configured power headroom to increase the power for beamforming UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

SigPowerIncreaseSwitch: Indicates whether to enable signaling power improvement. If this option is selected, the PDSCH transmit power increases when scheduling is performed for downlink retransmission of signaling during network entry. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

BFModeUserAdptPwrSwitch: Indicates whether to enable power adaptation for beamforming UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB adaptively increases the power of beamforming UEs in the cell based on the available power of the RRU and loads at neighboring cell edges. In this way, the average cell throughput increases. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

BfEnhChnPwrFormSwitch: Indicates whether to enable enhanced channel power forming for beamforming. If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines the TX power differences between antennas based on the Eigen beamforming weights of the antennas, determines the channel power headroom based on the percentage of idle RBs available in the current TTI in downlink scheduling, and flexibly allocates TX power to each channel of the RRU. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

DlNonStandardBwMaxPwrSwitch: If this option is deselected, the eNodeB determines the maximum transmit power of a cell of a non-standard bandwidth based on the number of available downlink RBs for a standard bandwidth. If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines the maximum transmit power of a cell of a non-standard bandwidth based on the number of available downlink RBs for a customized bandwidth. Changing the setting of this option will change the calculated total power and require cell re-activation. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PhichPwrRestrictSwitch: Indicates whether to enable PHICH power restriction. If this option is selected, the maximum number of UEs that can be scheduled in the uplink in each TTI is restricted by available PHICH power resources. If this option is deselected, the maximum number is not restricted by available PHICH power resources. This option takes effect only for non-massive-MIMO cells. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Uplink power control algorithm switch

Indicates whether to enable power control for the PUSCH or PUCCH. This parameter includes the following options:
