ComCoverUlCompSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL CoMP when multiple RRUs provide the same coverage in an SFN cell. UL CoMP is enabled for the SFN cell in which multiple RRUs provide the same coverage only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

SfnUplinkComp3CellSwitch: Indicates whether to enable three-cell UL CoMP for an SFN cell. Three-cell UL CoMP is enabled for the SFN cell only if this option is selected. The setting of this option is valid only if the IntraBaseBandSwitch or InterBaseBandSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Low Speed User Inter-frequency Handover Switch

Indicates whether to enable the inter-frequency directional handover algorithm for low-speed UEs. If this switch is turned on, the eNodeB instructs low-speed UEs in RRC_CONNECTED mode on high-speed railway networks to be handed over to low-speed inter-frequency neighboring cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Relay Switch

Indicates whether to enable relay-related functions.

OutOfBandRelaySwitch: Indicates whether to enable out-of-band relay. If this option is selected, out-of-band relay is enabled. If this option is deselected, out-of-band relay is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayDeNbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable in-band relay for the DeNB. If this option is selected, in-band relay is enabled for the DeNB. If this option is deselected, in-band relay is disabled for the DeNB. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayReNbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable in-band relay for the ReNB. If this option is selected, in-band relay is enabled for the ReNB. If this option is deselected, in-band relay is disabled for the ReNB. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InBandRelayDistantDeploySw: Indicates whether to allow the first-hop ReNB to be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. If this option is selected, the first-hop ReNB can be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. If this option is deselected, the first-hop ReNB cannot be deployed as far as 20 km from the DeNB in in-band relay scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Inter-frequency Directional Handover Switch

Indicates whether to enable the inter-frequency directional handover algorithm. If this switch is turned on, the eNodeB instructs UEs in RRC_CONNECTED mode on high-speed railway networks to measure inter-frequency cells so that the UEs can be handed over to high-speed inter-frequency cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Power Derating Switch

Indicates the switch used to determine which power derating scheme to use. If this parameter is set to OFF, the shared channel transmit power is reduced but the RS power, PSCH power, and power of other common channels are not reduced, which helps ensure coverage but decreases cell throughput. With this parameter setting, the configured RS power cannot exceed half of the maximum transmit power of the cell. For details about how to calculate the RS power and the maximum transmit power of a cell, see 3GPP TS 36.213. If this parameter is set to ON, both of the shared channel transmit power and RS power are reduced, which causes coverage to shrink but helps to maintain cell throughput. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
DetectionAlgoSwitch

CqiReliableSwitch: Indicates whether channel quality indicator (CQI) detection errors can be identified when interference occurs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can identify CQI detection errors when interference occurs and use a historical CQI. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB cannot identify CQI detection errors when interference occurs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DrxCqiFalseOptSwitch: Indicates whether to collect statistics about CQI values detected within the time specified by DrxInactivityTimer triggered by false SR detections. The eNodeB does not collect such statistics only when this option is selected.
Pucch IRC Enhance

Indicates whether the eNodeB determines interference strength. Determining interference strength ensures accurate adaptive switching between maximum ratio combining (MRC) and interference rejection combining (IRC). This parameter takes effect only when PucchIrcSwitch of the IrcSwitch parameter is turned on.

Turning on both this switch and PucchIrcSwitch at the same time provides better PUCCH detection performance than turning off both switches.

If PucchIrcSwitch is turned on, turning on this switch decreases the number of false detections and also slightly decreases PUCCH detection performance. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Ac Bar Algorithm Switch for Dynamic

Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring algorithms. This parameter controls whether to enable dynamic access barring on mobile-originated (MO) signaling, MO data, service-specific access control (SSAC) for multimedia telephony (MMTEL) voice services, or SSAC for MMTEL video services. This parameter takes effect only when the AcBarAlgoSwitch parameter in the CellAlgoSwitch MO is set to ACBAR_SWITCH_DYNAMIC.

DYNAMIC_FOR_MO: Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring on UEs running MO signaling or MO data.

If this switch is on, dynamic access barring applies to UEs running MO signaling or MO data.

If this switch is off, dynamic access barring does not apply to UEs running MO signaling or MO data. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DYNAMIC_FOR_SSAC: Indicates whether to enable dynamic access barring on UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services.

If this switch is on, dynamic access barring applies to UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services.

If this switch is off, dynamic access barring does not apply to UEs running MMTEL voice services or MMTEL video services. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
CRE Switch

Indicates whether the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect in a HetNet.

If this parameter is set to OFF and eICIC is enabled, the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect. If this parameter is set to OFF and eICIC is disabled, the CellRangeExpansion parameter does not take effect. If this parameter is set to ON, the CellRangeExpansion parameter takes effect for all UEs (including R8 and R9 UEs) in the cell. For macro and micro cells in a HetNet, it is recommended that this parameter be set to the same value. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Backoff Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable MLB for low-efficiency UEs.

InterFreqBackoffSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-neighboring-cell inter-frequency MLB for low-efficiency UEs. If this option is selected, inter-frequency MLB is enabled for low-efficiency UEs. If this option is deselected, inter-frequency MLB is disabled for low-efficiency UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
Handover Allowed Switch

Indicates whether to enable eNodeB-level handovers to be activated in the cell when related eNodeB-level handover switches are on.

SrvBasedInterFreqHoSw: Indicates whether to enable service-based inter-frequency handover to be activated in the cell. Service-based inter-frequency handover is allowed to be activated in the cell only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranSepOpMobilitySwitch: Indicates whether to differentiate mobility policies for GERAN operators. When this option is selected, different network policies can be applied to different GERAN operators. Such policies include SRVCC and SI obtaining through RIM procedures. When this option is deselected, different network policies cannot be applied to different GERAN operators. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranCsfbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSFB to UTRAN. When this option is selected, CSFB to UTRAN is allowed. When this option is deselected, the UTRAN CSFB algorithm is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranCsfbSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSFB to GERAN. When this option is selected, CSFB to GERAN is allowed. When this option is deselected, the GERAN CSFB algorithm is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranFlashCsfbSwitch: This option does not take effect if UtranCsfbSwitch is off. When UtranFlashCsfbSwitch is on, flash CSFB to UTRAN is enabled, and UTRAN system information is carried during redirection. When UtranFlashCsfbSwitch is off, flash CSFB to UTRAN is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranFlashCsfbSwitch: This option does not take effect if GeranCsfbSwitch is off. When GeranFlashCsfbSwitch is on, flash CSFB to GERAN is enabled, and GERAN system information is carried during redirection. When GeranFlashCsfbSwitch is off, flash CSFB to GERAN is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch: This option does not take effect if BlindHoSwitch is off. If BlindHoSwitch is on and UFCsfbBlindHoDisSwitch is also on, CsfbAdaptiveBlindHoSwitch does not take effect and UEs fall back to UTRAN or GERAN through ultra-flash CSFB. When this option is selected, CSFB adaptive blind handover is enabled. In this situation, an optimal handover mode is used depending on UE position in the CSFB scenario. When this option is deselected, CSFB adaptive blind handover is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranCsfbSteeringSwitch: UTRAN CSFB steering is enabled when this option is selected. In this case, RRC_IDLE UEs can use separate configuration policies when triggering CSFB. UTRAN CSFB steering is disabled when this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranCsfbSteeringSwitch: GERAN CSFB steering is enabled when this option is selected. In this case, RRC_IDLE UEs can use separate configuration policies when triggering CSFB. GERAN CSFB steering is disabled when this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServBasedHoBackSwitch: Indicates whether to allow service- or service-request-based inter-frequency handovers to source frequencies for UEs that have been handed over from the source frequencies to target frequencies. Such handovers to the source frequencies are allowed only if this option is selected. This switch takes effect for TDD cells with ServiceHoBearerPolicy set to SERVICE_HO_QCI_ALLOWED and does not take effect for TDD cells with ServiceHoBearerPolicy set to SERVICE_HO_QCI_NOT_ALLOWED. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch: If this option is selected, service-request-based inter-frequency handovers are enabled and UEs using a specific type of service can be handed over to inter-frequency cells. If this option is deselected, service-request-based inter-frequency handovers are disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

IdleCsfbRedirectOptSwitch: If this option is selected and the preferred CSFB policy is redirection, during CSFB of RRC_IDLE UEs, the eNodeB sends redirection messages without activating the security mode and performing RRC connection reconfiguration. However, if the eNodeB needs to query the UE capability information during redirection-based CSFB for RRC_IDLE UEs and the UeCapbEnquirySecurityEnhSwitch option of the ProtocolMsgOptSwitch parameter in the GlobalProcSwitch MO is selected, then the security mode activation, UE capability transfer, and RRC connection release procedures are processed in serial mode. In accordance with the security requirements of 3GPP CR (R2-1714284), this option takes effect only for redirections to UTRAN cells. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ServBasedNrHoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable service-based handover from E-UTRAN to NR. Service-based handover from E-UTRAN to NR is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Neighbor Cell Classification Mgt Switch

Indicates neighboring cell classification management. This parameter includes the following options:

INTRA_RAT_NCELL_CLASS_SW: If this option is selected, intra-RAT event-triggered ANR or intra-RAT fast ANR is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this frequency, the eNodeB automatically marks the NCellClassLabel parameter value as FORMAL or EXTENDED for intra-RAT neighboring cells based on handover-related statistic results. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NCELL_CLASS_HO_POLICY_SW: If this option is selected, intra-RAT event-triggered ANR is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this frequency, target cells for handovers must be determined based on E-UTRAN cell global identifier (ECGI) reading on intra-RAT neighboring cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NCELL_CLASS_PCI_CONFLICT_SW: If this option is selected and PCI conflict detection is enabled, PCI conflict detection and reporting are not performed on the neighboring cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UTRAN_NCELL_CLASS_SW: If this option is selected, event-triggered ANR with UTRAN or fast ANR with UTRAN is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this UTRAN frequency, the eNodeB automatically marks the NCellClassLabel parameter value as FORMAL or EXTENDED for neighboring UTRAN cells based on handover-related statistic results. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GERAN_NCELL_CLASS_SW: If this option is selected, event-triggered ANR with GERAN or fast ANR with GERAN is enabled, and the ANR indicator is set to ALLOWED for this GERAN frequency, the eNodeB automatically marks the NCellClassLabel parameter value as FORMAL or EXTENDED for neighboring GERAN cells based on handover-related statistic results. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EXTENDED_NCELL_MNG_SW: (1) For intra-RAT neighboring cells: If this option is selected, the eNodeB tries to write intra-RAT neighboring cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED in the memory to the configuration database when any of the following conditions is met: (a). The IntraRatNcellMgtMode parameter value is changed from INTERNAL_STORAGE_MODE to CONFIGURATION_MODE. (b). Intra-RAT event-triggered ANR and intra-RAT fast ANR are both disabled. (c). The INTRA_RAT_NCELL_CLASS_SW option is deselected. (d). The INTRA_RAT_ANR_SW option of the AnrFunctionSwitch parameter is deselected. (e) The ANR indicator is set to NOT_ALLOWED for an intra-RAT frequency. (f). The value of the OptMode parameter in the ANR MO is changed from FREE to CONTROLLED. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB directly discards intra-RAT neighboring cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED in the memory when any of the preceding conditions is met. (2) For neighboring UTRAN (or GERAN) cells: If this option is selected, the eNodeB tries to write neighboring UTRAN (or GERAN) cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED in the memory to the configuration database when any of the following conditions is met: (a). Event-triggered ANR with UTRAN (or GERAN) and fast ANR with UTRAN (or GERAN) are both disabled. (b). The UTRAN_NCELL_CLASS_SW (or GERAN_NCELL_CLASS_SW) option is deselected. (c). The INTER_RAT_ANR_SW option of the AnrFunctionSwitch parameter is deselected. (d). The ANR indicator is set to NOT_ALLOWED for the UTRAN (or GERAN) frequency. (e). The value of the OptMode parameter in the ANR MO is changed from FREE to CONTROLLED. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB directly discards neighboring UTRAN (or GERAN) cells whose NCellClassLabel parameter value is EXTENDED in the memory when any of the preceding conditions is met. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Specified PCI Based Policy Switch

Indicates whether to enable PCI-based algorithms. This parameter includes the following options:

IntraAnrPolicySwitch: Indicates whether to enable PCI-based intra-RAT ANR. If this option is selected and intra-RAT event-triggered ANR or intra-RAT fast ANR is enabled, PCI-based intra-RAT ANR is enabled to construct and optimize neighboring relationships between common cells and cells with specified PCIs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PciConflictDetectPolicySwitch: Indicates whether to enable PCI-based PCI conflict detection. If this option is selected and PCI confusion detection is enabled, PCI-based PCI conflict detection is enabled. If the eNodeB attempts to configure a cell with a specified PCI that operates on the same frequency and has the same PCI with any configured neighboring cell of a common cell as another neighboring cell of the common cell, PCI confusion detection is not performed on the cell with the specified PCI. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NCellAddProhibitPolicySwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB does not add cells with specified PCIs to the NRT or NCL when adding neighbor relationships. If this option is deselected, cells with specified PCIs can be added to the NRT or NCL. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

If the PCI of a cell is within the value range specified by parameters in EutranSpePciCell MOs, this cell is a cell with a specified PCI; otherwise, this cell is a common cell.
Cell Passive Inter-Modulation Interference Mitigation Switch

Indicates whether to enable passive intermodulation (PIM) interference mitigation in a cell.

This parameter applies only to macro eNodeBs. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Prach Joint Reception Switch

Indicates whether to enable joint reception on the PRACH for 16T16R LTE TDD networks. This parameter applies only to 16T16R LTE TDD networks. If this parameter is set to ON(On), joint reception is enabled on the PRACH. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), joint reception is disabled on the PRACH. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
FeICIC Switch

Indicates whether to enable the FeICIC algorithm. In a heterogeneous network (HetNet), this algorithm can be used together the Dynamic TDM eICIC algorithm and the CRE function to extend the micro cell coverage area and increase the number of UEs served by the micro cell. To support the FeICIC algorithm, set the EicicSwitch parameter to DYNAMIC in both the macro and micro cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Coordinated AMC Switch

Indicates whether to enable coordinated AMC for a cell.

UlCamcSw: Indicates whether to enable uplink coordinated AMC for a cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlInterEnbCamcSw: Indicates whether to enable uplink inter-eNodeB coordinated AMC for a cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
RU Ue Map Switch

For LampSite cells: indicates whether to enable MPMU-level UE identification. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the eNodeB generates the mapping between UEs and MPMUs for MPMU-level maintenance and commissioning based on CHRs and counters. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

For macro cells: indicates whether to enable RRU-level UE positioning. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the eNodeB reports RRU-level measurement results for positioning. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
High Speed Optimized Switch

Indicates whether to enable scheduling optimization in high-speed or high-speed railway scenarios.

InterfHighSpeedSW: Indicates whether coordinated scheduling and anti-interference are enabled in high-speed railway scenarios. If this option is selected, coordinated scheduling and anti-interference are enabled in high-speed railway scenarios and the eNodeB works with the eCoordinator to perform coordinated scheduling between public and dedicated networks. If this option is deselected, coordinated scheduling and anti-interference are disabled in high-speed railway scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

HighSpeedUserRedirectSwitch: Indicates whether high-speed UEs can be redirected to high-speed frequencies. It is recommended that this option be selected only for common cells adjacent to a high-speed railway. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DlAfcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable downlink AFC. This option applies only to high-speed multi-RRU combination cells. The eNodeB performs AFC for downlink data received by UEs in such cells, reducing the performance loss caused by high-speed mobility. If this option is selected, downlink AFC is enabled. If this option is deselected, downlink AFC is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed: Indicates whether to enable UE-speed-based differentiated processing in high-speed railway cells and interference avoidance of high-speed UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB identifies rate attributes of UEs in the cell, performs differentiated processing on functions and parameters related to such rate attributes, and supports interference avoidance of high-speed UEs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform differentiated processing on functions and parameters related to such rate attributes and interference avoidance of high-speed UEs does not take effect.

SpeedBasedMimoAdpSwitch: Indicates whether to enable rate-based differentiated adaptive MIMO for high-speed railway cells. If this option is selected, the eNodeB identifies rate attributes of UEs in the cell and performs differentiated processing on functions and parameters related to such rate attributes. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform differentiated processing on functions and parameters related to such rate attributes. This option takes effect only when the ProcSwitchBasedOnUserSpeed option of this parameter is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

FastAutoFreqControlSwitch: Indicates whether to quickly compensate for uplink frequency deviation for high-speed UEs.

HighSpeedVolteRedirectSwitch: Indicates whether to redirect high-speed VoLTE UEs to high-speed frequencies. It is recommended that this option be selected only for common cells adjacent to a high-speed railway. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LowSpeedBlindRedirect: Indicates whether to redirect low-speed UEs to low-speed frequencies. Select this option only for high-speed cells in high-speed railway scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HSCABlindRedirectSw: Indicates whether to perform inter-frequency blind redirections for high-speed CA UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

AfcEnhTimeOfsModPucchCfoEstSw: Indicates whether to enable enhanced time-domain offset modification for the PUSCH and CFO estimation for the PUCCH. This option takes effect only when the HighSpeedFlag parameter is set to HIGH_SPEED and the UlCyclicPrefix parameter is set to NORMAL_CP. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Service Differentiated Switch

Indicates whether to enable the functions related to service differentiation.

VideoRateAdaptSwitch: If this option is deselected, video service rate adaption is disabled. If this option is selected, video service rate adaption is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DownLoadRateLimitSwitch: If this option is deselected, busy-hour download rate control is disabled. If this option is selected, busy-hour download rate control is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoiceDataDifferentiationSwitch: Indicates whether to enable functions related to voice and data service differentiation. The functions are enabled only when this option is selected. The functions include TDD and FDD functions. TDD functions include those for CQI algorithms, PDCCH adjustment, exclusion of downlink QCI-5 services from the service scope of AMBR-based rate control, disabling the function controlled by DLDeltaCqiOptSwitch for SCCs of downlink CA UEs, CQI mask for QCI 1, and SCell deactivation based on the packet loss rate. FDD functions include exclusion of downlink QCI-5 services from the service scope of AMBR-based rate control, and disabling the function controlled by DLDeltaCqiOptSwitch for SCCs of downlink CA UEs, CQI mask for QCI 1, and SCell deactivation based on the packet loss rate. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

AsForTcpSw: Indicates whether to enable TCP link setup acceleration guarantee. If this option is selected, TCP link setup acceleration is enabled. If this option is deselected, TCP link setup acceleration is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LowDelayServiceOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable delay optimization for services that require low service delay. Delay optimization is enabled for these services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
PTT QoS Switch

Indicates whether to enable GBR for dedicated bearers allocated to PTT services with extended QCIs. GBR can be enabled for dedicated bearers allocated to PTT services in a cell only when this parameter is set to ON(On). This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
SRS and PUCCH Allocation Enhanced Switch

Indicates whether to enable allocation enhancement for SRS and PUCCH resources. When this parameter is set to ON, allocation enhancement for SRS and PUCCH resources is enabled and inaccurate resource allocation caused by the delay of cell load update no longer occurs. When this parameter is set to OFF, the general resource allocation procedure is used and inaccurate resource allocation caused by the delay of cell load update occurs. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
UE Inactive Timer for QCI1 Switch

Indicates how the UE inactivity timer takes effect for voice services. If the SpidUeInactivityTimer parameter in the SpidCfg MO is set to an invalid value, there is no SpidCfg MO configuration, or no SPID is specified for voice-service UEs, then the UE inactivity timer for voice services varies depending on the setting of the UEInactiveTimerQCI1Switch parameter as follows: If this parameter is set to ON, the UE inactivity timer for voice services is specified by the UeInactiveTimerForQci parameter in the QciPara MO corresponding to QCI 1. If this parameter is set to OFF, the UE inactivity timer for voice services is specified by the UeInactiveTimerForQci parameter in one of the involved QciPara MOs in which the Qci paramter is set to a value other than 1, and which of these MOs is applicable is determined based on the settings of the UeInactiveTimerPri parameter in these MOs. If the SpidUeInactivityTimer parameter in the SpidCfg MO is set to a valid value, the SpidUeInactivityTimer parameter value takes effect for voice services regardless of the setting of the UEInactiveTimerQCI1Switch parameter. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
UL JR Antenna Number Combined Switch

Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with the specific number of RX antennas.

Ul1R1RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with one RX antenna. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Ul1R2RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with one RX antenna and cells each with two RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Ul2R8RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with two RX antennas and cells each with eight RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

Ul4R8RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with four RX antennas and cells each with eight RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

Ul8R8RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with eight RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

When UL CoMP is enabled or switches related to joint reception in SFN cells are turned on, UL joint reception among cells each with two RX antennas and UL joint reception among cells each with four RX antennas are enabled by default.

Ul1R4RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with one RX antenna and cells each with four RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Ul2R4RJRSwitch: Indicates whether to enable UL joint reception among cells each with two RX antennas and cells each with four RX antennas. If this option is selected, UL joint reception among such cells is enabled. If this option is deselected, UL joint reception among such cells is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
VAM Phase Shift Algorithm Switch

VamPhaseShiftAlgoSwitch: This option applies to 4 antennas+2 ports and 8 antennas+2 ports scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
ANR Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable automatic neighbor relation (ANR). This parameter provides the following options:

BACKWARD_ANR_SW: When intra-RAT event-triggered ANR or intra-RAT fast ANR is enabled and the BACKWARD_ANR_SW option is selected for the target cell of an ANR procedure, the cell can add the source cell to its NCL and NRT after obtaining source cell information. If BACKWARD_ANR_SW is deselected, the target cell does not add the source cell to its NCL or NRT. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

INTRA_RAT_OVERDISTANCE_SW: If this option is selected and intra-RAT neighboring cell classification takes effect in memory mode, the eNodeB reports information about intra-RAT neighboring cells to the MAE for the MAE to detect over-distant neighboring cells and handles the detection result returned by the MAE. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not report information about intra-RAT neighboring cells to the MAE or handle the over-distant neighboring cell detection result returned by the MAE. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UTRAN_OVERDISTANCE_SW: If this option is selected and neighboring UTRAN cell classification takes effect, the eNodeB reports information about neighboring UTRAN cells to the MAE for the MAE to detect over-distant neighboring UTRAN cells and handles the detection result returned by the MAE. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not report information about neighboring UTRAN cells to the MAE or handle the over-distant neighboring UTRAN cell detection result returned by the MAE. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LTE_AUTON_GAP_CGI_SW: If this option is selected, the eNodeB instructs UEs that support autonomous gaps to use autonomous gaps to read the CGIs of neighboring cells. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not instruct such UEs to use autonomous gaps to read the CGIs of neighboring cells. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Downlink 256QAM Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable the algorithms related to downlink (DL) 256 quadrature amplitude modulation (256QAM).

Dl256QamSwitch: Indicates whether to enable DL 256QAM. If this option is selected, DL 256QAM is enabled, and the highest-order DL modulation scheme is 256QAM for UEs that support DL 256QAM. If this option is deselected, DL 256QAM is disabled and the highest-order DL modulation scheme is 64QAM. This feature takes effect only when a UBBP or LBBPd board is configured. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

Dl256QamAccessorySwitch: Indicates whether to enable DL 256QAM accessory. DL 256QAM accessory is enabled only if this option is selected. When this option is selected, error vector magnitude (EVM) is optimized for 256QAM in the TTIs in which DL 256QAM takes effect. The function takes effect only when a UBBP board is configured. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

Dl256QamTbsIndexExtSwitch: Indicates whether TBS index 33B can be used for DL 256QAM UEs. TBS index 33B can be used only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
MLB Auto Group Configuration Switch

OverlapIndAutoSwitch: Indicates whether the serving cell evaluates its coverage overlap with neighboring cells and adds or removes coverage overlap flags for the neighboring cells. The serving cell performs these actions only when this option is selected. The settings of MicroOverlapIndSwitch and MicroGroupOverlapIndSwitch take effect only when the OverlapIndAutoSwitch option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MicroOverlapIndSwitch: Indicates whether a micro cell evaluates its coverage overlap with the associated macro cell and instructs the macro cell to add or remove the coverage overlap flag for the micro cell. The micro cell performs these actions only when this option is selected. If OverlapIndAutoSwitch and MicroOverlapIndSwitch both are selected for a macro cell, the macro cell adds or removes the coverage overlap flag for a micro cell when instructed to do so by the micro cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MicroGroupOverlapIndSwitch: Indicates whether a macro cell evaluates its coverage overlap with a group of associated micro cells and adds or removes coverage overlap flags for all micro cells in the group. The macro cell performs these actions only when this option is selected. This option applies only to macro cells. If both MicroOverlapIndSwitch and MicroGroupOverlapIndSwitch are selected for a macro cell, only MicroOverlapIndSwitch takes effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
CA Auto Group Configuration Switch

Indicates whether to enable automatic grouping for carrier aggregation. If automatic grouping is enabled, the eNodeB identifies the proportion of overlapping coverage between the serving and inter-frequency neighboring cells and automatically adds, modifies, or removes the blind SCell configuration flag for the inter-frequency neighboring cells. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Attach Cell Auto Config Switch

Indicates whether to enable automatic configuration of macro-micro associations. If this parameter is set to ON, a micro cell performs measurements to determine the associated macro cell. The macro-micro association is updated at the specified time. If this parameter is set to OFF, macro-micro associations cannot be automatically configured. Automatic configuration of macro-micro associations requires that this parameter be set to ON for all macro and micro cells involved. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Downlink Cell Coverage Enhancement Switch

Indicates whether to enable the two functions related to downlink coverage enhancement in the cell.

DlCellCoverEnhanceAlgoSw: Indicates whether to enable cell coverage enhancement. After this function is enabled, the throughput of cell-edge UEs with rank-1 transmissions for which low MCS indexes are used increases in light-load wide-coverage scenarios, and the coverage capability of the cell improves. This function requires that the RRU serving the cell have power headroom, work in FDD-only mode, and be configured with a single carrier. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

AdptCellEdgePwrAllocSw: Indicates whether to enable adaptive power matching. Enabling this function in a lightly loaded wide coverage cell helps improve the performance of CEUs. This function is enabled only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
Uplink Schedule Extended Switch

Indicates whether to enable extended functions of uplink scheduling.

UlPacketLenAwareSchSw: Indicates whether to optimize uplink packet length awareness performance. Packet length awareness performance is optimized in the uplink only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EnhExtQCITtiBundlingSwitch: Indicates whether to enable TTI bundling for push-to-talk (PTT) services with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs. If this option is selected, the number of transmission opportunities increases within the air interface delay budget of PTT services, and uplink coverage is improved. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EnhExtQCISpsSchSwitch: Indicates whether to enable semi-persistent scheduling during talk spurts of PTT services with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs. If this option is selected, semi-persistent scheduling is applied. If this option is deselected, dynamic scheduling is applied. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlVoipRbRsvSwitch: Indicates whether to reserve PUSCH RBs for UEs running voice services in the uplink. PUSCH RBs are reserved for these UEs only if this option is selected. This option does not take effect if the uplink cell bandwidth is less than 5 MHz. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlThpEnhSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the enhanced uplink throughput improvement function. For TDD cells: If this option is selected, the eNodeB calculates the PUCCH overhead based on the actual number of RBs occupied by the PUCCH when the number of UEs is small in the cell, which improves the uplink throughput. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB calculates the PUCCH overhead based on the current PUCCH resource configuration. For an NB-IoT cell: If this option is selected, the eNodeB searches for NPUSCH resources first in the frequency domain and then in the time domain for uplink scheduling. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB searches for NPUSCH resources first in the time domain and then in the frequency domain for uplink scheduling. This option applies only to LTE TDD and NB-IoT.

UlDataFitterRbCalcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the algorithm of RB calculation based on uplink data volume matching. The algorithm takes effect only when this option and the SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter are selected. When this algorithm takes effect, the number of required RBs is estimated based on the UE capability and the data volume to be scheduled. If this algorithm does not take effect, the number of required RBs is estimated based on the UE spectral efficiency and the data volume to be scheduled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

UlFssOptForAttach: For TDD cells, this option indicates whether to perform uplink frequency selective scheduling based on the interference level during network access. If this option is selected, uplink frequency selective scheduling is performed based on the interference level during network access. If this option is deselected, uplink frequency selective scheduling is performed based on the SINR during network access. This option can be selected when the ULFSSAlgoSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter is selected. For NB-IoT cells, this option indicates whether to optimize SINR estimation during network access in multi-carrier scenarios. If this option is selected, SINR estimation will be optimized during network access in multi-carrier scenarios. During RRC connection setup, the SINR of the assigned carrier is estimated based on the check result during network access and the channel measurement on the assigned carrier. If this option is deselected, the SINR is estimated based only on the channel measurement on the assigned carrier during RRC connection setup. This option applies only to LTE TDD and NB-IoT.

SrbProbeSchSwitch: For FDD and TDD cells, this option indicates whether to proactively schedule uplink signaling. If this option is selected, the eNodeB triggers proactive scheduling of uplink signaling for once when the eNodeB determines that data correctly scheduled in the downlink is control-plane signaling that requires uplink signaling feedback and does not receive the feedback within a specified period of time. For NB-IoT cells, this option indicates whether to perform retransmissions in proactive scheduling of status reports. If this option is selected, the eNodeB triggers HARQ retransmissions in proactive scheduling of status reports in the same way as it triggers HARQ retransmissions in normal data scheduling. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform HARQ retransmissions in proactive scheduling of status reports.

UlSchCtrlPwrUserSetOptSw: Indicates whether to enable the algorithm of selecting UE sets for the scheduler-controlled power function. This algorithm takes effect only when this option and the SchedulerCtrlPowerSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter are selected. When this algorithm takes effect, pre-scheduled UEs of a lower priority are not included in the UE set selected by the scheduler. When this algorithm does not take effect, all pre-scheduled UEs are included in the UE set selected by the scheduler. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlPttSchOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable dynamic scheduling for PTT services with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs. If this option is selected, for PTT service UEs that are scheduled dynamically in the uplink, the eNodeB triggers uplink dynamic scheduling when the scheduling interval is greater than the uplink voice scheduling interval threshold. This ensures timely uplink scheduling for voice service users in heavy traffic scenarios, preventing packet discarding upon the timeout of PDCP packet discarding timer. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not trigger uplink dynamic scheduling for PTT services in the preceding scenario. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlPttDataSizeEstSwitch: Indicates whether to estimate uplink traffic volume for dynamic scheduling of PTT services with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB estimates uplink traffic volume for dynamic scheduling of PTT services, reducing the packet delay and packet loss rate and increasing voice quality of PTT services. If this option is deselected, the function does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

EnhancedSchForSparseSwitch: Indicates whether ping packets are subject to the preallocation bandwidth percentage restriction and whether to change the method of calculating the number of RBs to be allocated for SR-based dynamic scheduling of ping packets. If this option is selected, ping packets are not subject to the preallocation bandwidth percentage restriction, and the number of RBs to be allocated for SR-based dynamic scheduling of ping packets is calculated based on the size of the ping packets. If this option is deselected, ping packets are subject to the preallocation bandwidth percentage restriction, and the number of RBs to be allocated for SR-based dynamic scheduling of ping packets is calculated based on the average spectral efficiency. For FDD, this option indicates only whether ping packets are subject to the preallocation bandwidth percentage restriction. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlSigSrSchDateLenAdapt: Indicates whether to enable data amount adaptation for SR-based scheduling of uplink signaling. The adaptation is enabled only if this option is selected. When the adaptation is enabled, the eNodeB adaptively allocates the amount of to-be-scheduled data and the number of RBs based on the signaling identification result in SR-based scheduling of uplink signaling. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlFssWindSearchDirRandSw: Indicates whether to randomize the search window direction in uplink frequency selective scheduling. The randomization is enabled only if this option is selected. When the randomization is enabled, the eNodeB randomly specifies a search window direction for a cell in frequency selective scheduling. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

UlCellCenterMCSOptSwitch: For FDD and TDD cells, this option indicates whether to optimize MCSs for uplink transmissions by UEs in the cell center. The optimization is enabled only if this option is selected. When the optimization is enabled, the throughput of UEs running full-buffer services in the cell center or with medium distances to the cell center increases. For NB-IoT cells, this option indicates whether to optimize MCS selection for uplink transmissions by UEs in the cell center. The optimization is enabled only if this option is selected. When the optimization is enabled, the number of repetitions in common data scheduling for UEs in the cell center decreases.

PuschSchEscPucchSwitch: If this option is selected, PUCCH interference coordination takes effect, reducing PUCCH interference. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

HarqMcsOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the instantaneous DMRS measurement value can be used to select an MCS index for retransmission. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

PreambleSchEnhSwitch: If this option is selected, the preamble scheduling and demodulation success rates increase. If this option is deselected, the preamble scheduling and demodulation success rates do not increase. If this option is deselected in NB-IoT scenarios, the maximum backoff index included in MAC PDU is 12. If this option is selected in NB-IoT scenarios, the maximum backoff index included in MAC PDU is 7. This accommodates certain UEs not supporting higher backoff indexes.

UlPAMCSwitch: For FDD and TDD cells, this option indicates whether to enable the algorithm of AMC maximizing the uplink perceived throughput. If this option is selected, the algorithm takes effect and AMC adjustment and MCS index selection are performed in an optimized way. For an NB-IoT cell, this option indicates whether to enable AMC optimization for uplink scheduling. If this option is selected, uplink scheduling is performed based on the rule that the spectral efficiency is preferentially ensured. If this option is deselected, uplink scheduling is performed based on the rule that the coverage performance is preferentially ensured.

UlSchRbAllocOptSw: For FDD and TDD cells, this option indicates whether to enable RB allocation optimization in uplink scheduling. If this option is selected, PUSCH scheduling considers UE power status when the uplink resource block (RB) usage is less than or equal to 30%. In addition, uplink power control considers SINR fluctuation. Meanwhile, the scheduling priority precision increases. If this option is deselected, PUSCH scheduling does not consider UE power status or RB usage. In addition, uplink power control does not consider SINR fluctuation. Meanwhile, the scheduling priority precision decreases. For an NB-IoT cell, this option indicates whether resources are allocated on a per carrier or cell basis when multi-carrier scheduling is enabled. If this option is selected, resources are allocated on a per carrier basis. If this option is deselected, resources are allocated on a per cell basis.

UlMultiClusterSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the uplink multi-cluster function. This function is enabled only when the option is selected. Uplink discontinuous resource allocation is supported when this function is enabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

SrPairingSwitch: Indicates whether UEs can be paired for MU-MIMO-based SR transmission. If this option is selected, UEs can be paired for MU-MIMO-based SR transmission. If this option is deselected, UEs cannot be paired for MU-MIMO-based SR transmission. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlDrbProactiveSchSwitch: Indicates whether to enable proactive uplink scheduling. If this option is selected, proactive uplink scheduling is triggered once when only CQI-only scheduling is performed in the uplink within a period after downlink data transmission. If this option is deselected, proactive uplink scheduling is not triggered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
User Data Compression Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable the uplink data compression algorithm in a cell.

BasicUdcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the basic uplink data compression algorithm in the cell. Basic uplink data compression algorithm is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlHCodeSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the uplink HCode data compression algorithm in the cell. The uplink HCode data compression algorithm is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
VoLTE Switch

Indicates whether to enable VoLTE-related algorithms. AcBarForVoLTEPreferSwitch: Indicates whether to allow preferential access of voice services during access class control. If this option is selected, voice preference information (specified by the VoLTEPreferCfgInd parameter in the CellAcBar MO) is included in SIB2. This ensures that AC barring does not take effect at the access layer for UEs initiating any of the following services: MMTEL voice, MMTEL video, IP-based SMS, and SMS. If this option is deselected, SIB2 does not contain any voice preference information and AC barring takes effect for these UEs at the access layer. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoLTEMoPrefSwitch: Indicates whether to perform preferential processing for UEs that originate VoLTE services. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform preferential processing for these UEs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB optimizes procedures such as admission, scheduling, and flow control for these UEs to increase the VoLTE service success rate. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoLTEOffOptSwitch: Indicates whether to reject the setup of QCI 1 bearers for optimization when VoLTE is disabled. If this option is selected, the eNodeB rejects the setup of QCI 1 bearers (and QCI 2 bearers if there are QCI 2 videotelephony services) but accepts the setup of other bearers during initial context setup, bearer setup, or bearer modification procedures. This option takes effect only if the ServiceReqInterFreqHoSwitch option of the HoAlgoSwitch parameter in the ENodeBAlgoSwitch MO is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoLTECallConnDelayOptSw: Call connection delay optimization for UEs running voice services takes effect only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
VoIP Decryption Failure Self Recovery Switch

Indicates whether to enable self-rectification on voice decryption failures. When voice decryption fails due to disordered PDCP SNs, enabling this function allows detection and self-rectification on voice decryption failures, and disabling this function does not allow detection and self-rectification on voice decryption failures. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Deprioritisation Deliver Indicator

Indicates whether the RRCConnectionReject message includes the IE deprioritisationReq and the setting of the IE deprioritisationType. If this parameter is set to UNDELIVER(UNDELIVER), the RRCConnectionReject message does not include the IE deprioritisationReq. If this parameter is set to FREQUENCY(FREQUENCY), the RRCConnectionReject message includes the IE deprioritisationReq and the value of the IE deprioritisationType is FREQUENCY. In this case, after the UE receives an RRCConnectionReject message, the lowest cell reselection priority is configured for the serving frequency and the frequencies contained in the RRCConnectionReject messages that have been received and include the IE deprioritisationReq. If this parameter is set to EUTRA(EUTRA), the RRCConnectionReject message includes the IE deprioritisationReq and the value of the IE deprioritisationType is EUTRA. In this case, after the UE receives an RRCConnectionReject message, the lowest cell reselection priority is configured for all E-UTRAN frequencies. For details about the IEs, see 3GPP TS 36.331. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Cmas Switch

Indicates whether to enable commercial mobile alert system (CMAS). This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
MFBI Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable MFBI-related functions.

MFBIBandAdjAlgoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable priority adjustment for a secondary MFBI frequency band. If this option is selected, priority adjustment is enabled for a secondary MFBI frequency band. If this option is deselected, priority adjustment is disabled for a secondary MFBI frequency band. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MFBIX2HoSwitch: Indicates whether MFBI enhancement supports X2-based handovers. If this option is selected, MFBI enhancement supports X2-based handovers, and the FreqBandIndicatorPriority IE of the local cell can be delivered over the X2 interface. If this option is deselected, MFBI enhancement does not support X2-based handovers, and the FreqBandIndicatorPriority IE of the local cell cannot be delivered over the X2 interface. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MFBIFixedS1HoSwitch: Indicates the switch used to control handover mode selection in multi-band scenarios. If this option is selected, handovers are always performed based on the S1 interface in multi-band scenarios. If this option is deselected, handovers are handled as originally in multi-band scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
PCI Conflict Report Switch

Indicates whether the eNodeB reports physical cell identifier (PCI) conflicts to the MAE.

COLLISION_REPORT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, check results about whether PCI collisions occur between the local cell and other cells served by the same base station as the local cell or between the local cell and external cells are reported to the MAE. If this option is deselected, these check results are not reported to the MAE. For details about PCI collisions, see 3GPP TS 36.902. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CONFUSION_REPORT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, check results about whether PCI confusions occur between candidate target neighboring cells whose neighbor relationships with the local cell are recorded in the NRTs are reported to the MAE. If this option is deselected, these check results are not reported to the MAE. For details about PCI confusion, see 3GPP TS 36.902. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
MRO algorithm switch

Indicates the options for mobility robustness optimization (MRO) algorithms.

IntraFreqMroSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the intra-frequency handover parameters to increase the intra-frequency handover success rate. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is not performed. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

InterFreqMroSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the inter-frequency handover parameters to increase the inter-frequency handover success rate. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is not performed. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranMroSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the UTRAN handover parameters to increase the success rate of handovers to UTRAN. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is not performed. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranMroSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the GERAN handover parameters to increase the success rate of handovers to GERAN. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is not performed. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UEMroSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the UE-level handover parameters to decrease the number of ping-pong handovers, thereby increasing the UEs' handover success rate. If this option is deselected, the adjustment is not performed. In this version, the function of preventing ping-pong handovers in the UE-level MRO algorithm takes effect only in intra-frequency handover scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

IntraFreqReselOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts intra-frequency cell reselection parameters, which reduces the number of unnecessary handovers and ensures a steady handover success rate. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not adjust intra-frequency cell reselection parameters.

UtranUnnecHoOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts parameters about handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN to reduce the number of unnecessary handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN, increase the probability that UEs camp in LTE networks, and improve user experience. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform the adjustment. For LTE FDD, this option takes effect only when the UnnecHoOptWithoutRIM option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GeranUnnecHoOptSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts parameters about handovers from E-UTRAN to GERAN to reduce the number of unnecessary handovers from E-UTRAN to GERAN, increase the probability that UEs camp in LTE networks, and improve user experience. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform the adjustment. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

UnnecHoOptWithoutRIM: If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses a Huawei proprietary non-RIM-based solution to reduce the number of unnecessary inter-RAT handovers. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB uses the protocol-defined RIM-based solution to reduce the number of unnecessary inter-RAT handovers. LTE FDD supports only the protocol-defined scheme with RIM. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Operator Resource Group Share Switch

Indicates whether to enable RAN sharing so that admission and scheduling take effect in RAN sharing mode. When the switch is on, operators share the resources specified by the RAN-sharing operator group. When the OpPrbGroupshareSwitch option is selected, PRB resources can be shared by operator groups. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Cell Recovery Switch

NoUserCellRecoverySwitch: Indicates whether the eNodeB checks the number of served UEs in a cell when reestablishing the cell. If this option is selected, the eNodeB does not check whether there are UEs in a cell and directly reestablishes the cell with a higher cell specification. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB checks whether there are UEs in a cell and reestablishes the cell with a higher cell specification only if there are no UEs in the cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Enhanced MLB Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable MLB algorithm enhancements.

SpectralEffBasedLoadEvalSw: If this option is deselected, spectral efficiency differences are not considered during load comparison between the local cell and inter-frequency neighboring cells for intra-RAT user-number-based MLB. If this option is selected, spectral efficiency differences are considered in the preceding scenario, and cells with high spectral efficiency process more traffic. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VideoSrvLoadCtrlSw: If this option is deselected, handovers are not triggered for video service users based on the video service load in a cell. If this option is selected, handovers are triggered for video service users based on the video service load in a cell, improving video service experience. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CaUserLoadTransferSw: If this option is deselected, a CA UE is considered as one UL-sync UE separately in the PCell and SCell for cell-level MLB, and RRC_CONNECTED CA UEs configured with SCells cannot be transferred. If this option is selected, a CA UE is considered as one UL-sync UE only in the PCell for cell-level MLB, RRC_CONNECTED CA UEs configured with SCells can be transferred during user-number-based MLB. In addition, the PCC priority is considered, and a higher priority is allocated to the serving frequency when CA UEs with RRC connections released are to be transferred. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DediPrioManageOnLowLoadSw: If this option is deselected, UEs in lightly loaded cells do not carry the dedicated cell-reselection frequency priorities generated based on cell loads when the RRC connections of the UEs are released. If this option is selected and the eNodeB detects a different frequency with heavy loads, UEs in lightly-loaded cells carry the priorities when the RRC connections of the UEs are released, and the dedicated cell-reselection priority for the heavy-load frequency is lowered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlHeavyTrafficBaseLoadSw: If this option is deselected, loads of target FDD cells are not considered during uplink traffic-based MLB. If this option is selected, loads of target FDD cells are considered during uplink traffic-based MLB. If a target LTE FDD cell is heavily loaded, UEs cannot be handed over to this cell. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlExperiBasedTrafficSteerSw: If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not transfer UEs with heavy uplink traffic based on uplink experience. If this option is selected, the eNodeB transfers UEs with heavy uplink traffic based on uplink experience, improving uplink user experience. This function takes effect only when the FreqSelectStrategy parameter is set to FAIRSTRATEGY. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlTrafficSteeringSw: If this option is deselected, the eNodeB transfers UEs regardless of whether uplink traffic is heavy or not during intra-RAT inter-duplex MLB. If this option is selected, the eNodeB does not transfer UEs with large uplink traffic volumes during intra-RAT inter-duplex MLB, allowing LTE FDD networks to handle more uplink services. This option takes effect only when the FreqSelectStrategy parameter in the CellMLB MO is set to FAIRSTRATEGY. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

ActiveUeBasedLoadEvalSw: If this option is selected, the eNodeB considers the difference in the number of active UEs when comparing loads of inter-frequency cells for intra-RAT user-number-based MLB. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB considers the difference of the number of uplink-synchronized UEs when comparing loads of inter-frequency cells for intra-RAT user-number-based MLB. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DediPrioManageOnLowLoadEnhSw: If this option is selected and the eNodeB detects a non-serving E-UTRA frequency with heavy loads, UEs in lightly-loaded cells carry the dedicated cell-reselection frequency priorities generated based on the cell loads when the RRC connections of the UEs are released (not only those caused by the UE Inactivity Timer timeout), and the dedicated priority for the heavy-load frequency is lowered.

LoadTransferEnhSw: If this option is selected, based on the frequency priorities and load differences, the eNodeB increases the upper limits of the numbers of UEs that can be transferred by idle mode MLB and connected mode MLB. In addition, the target for PRB-usage-based MLB is changed from balancing of the number of remaining PRBs to balancing of PRB usage. If this option is deselected, this algorithm does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

NCellTrigThldSmartOptAlgoSw: Indicates whether to enable smart optimization of neighboring-cell-specific MLB triggering thresholds. If this option is selected, the eNodeB sets a dedicated MLB triggering threshold for each inter-frequency neighboring cell of the serving cell, and checks whether the triggering thresholds are reached based on the load status of the serving and inter-frequency neighboring cells. Only the candidate cells that meet the condition are selected for MLB. In addition, the eNodeB periodically constructs a prediction model for the MLB triggering threshold to predict an optimum value for the neighboring-cell-specific MLB triggering threshold and change the threshold to the predicted value. If this option is deselected, this algorithm does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Traffic MLB Switch

Indicates whether to enable traffic-based MLB algorithms.

UlHeavyTrafficMlbSwitch: Indicates whether to select UEs with heavy uplink traffic for MLB. If this option is selected, UEs with heavy uplink traffic are selected for MLB. These UEs are handed over to the target neighboring cell. If this option is deselected and the UlHeavyTrafficMlbTFCASw option is selected, only FDD+TDD CA UEs with heavy uplink traffic are selected for MLB. If both this option and the UlHeavyTrafficMlbTFCASw option are deselected, UEs with heavy uplink traffic are not selected for MLB. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

UlHeavyTrafficMlbTFCASw: Indicates whether to select FDD+TDD CA UEs with heavy uplink traffic for MLB. If this option is selected, FDD+TDD CA UEs with heavy uplink traffic are selected for MLB. These UEs are handed over to the target neighboring cell. If this option is deselected and the UlHeavyTrafficMlbSwitch option is selected, UEs with heavy uplink traffic are selected for MLB and are handed over to the target neighboring cell. If both this option and the UlHeavyTrafficMlbSwitch option are deselected, FDD+TDD CA UEs with heavy uplink traffic are not selected for MLB. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
MTC Switch

Indicates whether to enable machine type communication (MTC).

If this parameter is set to ON(On), category 0 UEs can access the cell.

If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), category 0 UEs cannot access the cell.

This parameter applies only to LTE FDD.
Uplink Interference Coordination Switch

Indicates whether to enable the uplink interference coordination algorithm. If the UL_ICS_SWITCH(UlIcSwitch) option is deselected, the uplink interference coordination algorithm does not take effect. If the UL_ICS_SWITCH(UlIcSwitch) option is selected, the uplink interference coordination algorithm is enabled. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
FCS Mode

Indicates the frequency channel selection (FCS) mode of an LAA cell. If this parameter is set to MANUAL(MANUAL), the working frequency of an LAA cell is manually configured. If this parameter is set to ONCE(ONCE), the eNodeB detects interference on the unlicensed spectrum and automatically configures the working frequency of the LAA cell. This operation is performed only once after configuration parameters are delivered. If this parameter is set to AUTO(AUTO), the eNodeB detects interference on the unlicensed spectrum and automatically configures the working frequency of the LAA cell. This operation is performed once after configuration parameters are delivered and is automatically triggered when certain conditions are met. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
Video Opt Switch

Indicates whether to enable video experience optimization. SC_VIDEO_RATE_ADAPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable rate adaptation for video services. Rate adaptation is enabled for video services only if this option is selected. VOD_SERVICE_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable VOD optimization. VOD optimization is enabled only if this option is selected. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
SPID Specific Handover Switch

Indicates the switches that control SPID-based handover.

INTER_FREQ_LIST_CTL_SW: This option controls SPID-based target frequency list in inter-frequency handovers. If this option is selected and an RatFreqPriorityGroup MO is configured for an SPID, UEs with the SPID can be handed over to cells on the frequency indicated in the RatFreqPriorityGroup MO in inter-frequency handovers. If this option is deselected, the target frequency for UEs with the SPID in inter-frequency handovers is not controlled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

L2U_FREQ_LIST_CTL_SW: This option controls SPID-based target frequency list in E-UTRAN-to-UTRAN handovers and redirections. If this option is selected and an RatFreqPriorityGroup MO is configured for an SPID, UEs with the SPID can be handed over or redirected to UTRAN cells on the frequency indicated in the RatFreqPriorityGroup MO in E-UTRAN-to-UTRAN handovers or redirections. If this option is deselected, the target frequency for UEs with the SPID in E-UTRAN-to-UTRAN handovers and redirections is not controlled. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
D-MIMO Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable D-MIMO algorithms. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

DmimoJTSwitch: Indicates whether to enable D-MIMO joint transmission. If this option is selected, D-MIMO joint transmission is enabled. If this option is deselected, D-MIMO joint transmission is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

DmimoSchAttriOptSwitch: Indicates whether to enable scheduling attribute optimization for D-MIMO UEs. If this option is selected, scheduling attribute optimization is enabled for D-MIMO UEs. If this option is deselected, scheduling attribute optimization is disabled for D-MIMO UEs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

InterCellDmimoJTSwitch: Indicates whether to enable inter-cell D-MIMO. If this option is selected, inter-cell D-MIMO is enabled. If this option is deselected, inter-cell D-MIMO is disabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
Uplink Rate Control Mode

Indicates the uplink voice rate control mode.

If this parameter is set to ULRATECTRL_OFF, uplink voice rate control does not take effect. If RTP packets are encrypted, this parameter must be set to ULRATECTRL_OFF.

If this parameter is set to ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink voice AMR control.

If this parameter is set to ULAMRC_SBC_CONTROL, the session border controller (SBC) on the EPC executes uplink voice AMR control. The SBC must be a Huawei SBC supporting voice AMR control.

If this parameter is set to ULEVSC_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink EVS rate control.

If this parameter is set to ADAPTIVE_ENB_CONTROL, the eNodeB executes uplink AMR/EVS adaptive coding rate control. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Rate Control Algorithm Switch

Indicates the switches of algorithm policies used for adjusting the uplink speech rate and related functions.

UlAmrcExceedingInitialSw: Indicates whether the adjusted uplink AMR voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate for this call. The adjusted uplink AMR voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlAmrCheckSw: Indicates whether to adjust the uplink AMR voice coding rate if the eNodeB has not obtained the rate set information about this session and the UlAmrcMode parameter is set to ULAMRC_ENB_CONTROL. The uplink AMR voice coding rate can be adjusted only if this option is deselected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoiceCodingModeMeasSw: Indicates whether to measure the distribution of the numbers of uplink and downlink voice packets in different voice coding schemes on a per cell basis. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not measure the distribution of the numbers of uplink and downlink voice packets in different voice coding schemes on a per cell basis. If this option is selected, the corresponding counter is incremented based on the frame types (voice frame and silence frame in different voice coding schemes) each time the eNodeB PDCP layer receives an uplink or downlink PDCP SDU with a QCI of 1 in a cell. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

VoLTELoadAmrcSw: Indicates whether to enable voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control. If voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control is enabled, the AMRC/EVS rate is reduced when voice load of the cell is high. Voice-load-based AMRC/EVS rate control is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlEvsExceedingInitialSw: Indicates whether the adjusted uplink EVS voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate for this call. The adjusted uplink EVS voice coding rate can exceed the initial coding rate only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UlMcpttVoiceAmrcSw: Indicates whether to enable AMRC for uplink MCPTT voice services. AMRC is enabled for uplink MCPTT voice services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
CRS Interference Cancellation Switch

Indicates whether to deliver information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference. CRS-IC is independent from FeICIC. CRS interference can be canceled on a per subframe basis.

If this parameter is set to ON, CRS-IC is enabled and the eNodeB delivers information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference.

If this parameter is set to OFF, CRS-IC is disabled and the eNodeB does not deliver information about intra-frequency neighboring cells for UEs to cancel CRS interference. Therefore, UEs cannot cancel CRS interference. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Enhanced Channel Calibration Switch

Indicates whether to enable enhanced channel calibration.

QUICK_CHN_CAL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable fast channel calibration. If this option is selected, fast channel calibration is enabled. If this option is deselected, fast channel calibration is disabled. This option takes effect only when UBBP boards are used and does not take effect in the case of LBBP boards or in massive MIMO scenarios. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

TDD_PASSIVE_PORT_CAL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable channel calibration for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. If this option is selected, channel calibration takes effect for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. If this option is deselected, channel calibration does not take effect for passive ports of LTE TDD AAUs. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
NB-IoT Cell Algorithm Switch

Indicates the NB-IoT cell-level algorithm switches.

IDLE_EDRX_SWITCH: Indicates whether eDRX paging is supported. eDRX paging can be delivered in the local cell only when this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

COVERAGE_EXTENSION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the NB-IoT cell coverage extension function. When this option is selected, the NB-IoT cell coverage extension function is supported, and the eNodeB supports coverage extension through scheduling. When this option is deselected, common coverage is supported, which means that coverage extension cannot be achieved. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

MULTITONE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the NB-IoT Multi-tone feature. For uplink data transmission, when this option is selected, an eNodeB can allocate multiple subcarriers at a time to a multi-tone-capable UE. When this option is deselected, the Multi-tone feature is not supported, and the eNodeB allocates only one subcarrier at a time to a UE for uplink data transmission. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

SI_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_CFG_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the adaptive configuration of the NB-IoT system information (SI) frame offset. If this option is selected, adaptive configuration takes effect for NB-IoT SI frame offset. If this option is deselected, adaptive configuration does not take effect for NB-IoT SI frame offset, and the NB-IoT SI frame offset is fixed and cannot be adaptively configured. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DUAL_HARQ_SWITCH: Indicates whether the eNodeB supports dual-HARQ-process scheduling. If this option is selected, the eNodeB supports dual-HARQ-process scheduling and performs dual-HARQ-process scheduling for dual-HARQ-capable UEs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB supports only single-HARQ-process scheduling. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

ADAPTIVE_STEP_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable adaptive step adjustment in the NB-IoT cell. If this option is selected, the eNodeB uses a large step to adjust AMC to accelerate IBLER convergence when it determines that large-step adjustment is required based on HARQ feedback from the UE. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB uses a fixed step to adjust AMC. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

RETRIEVE_UE_INFO_SWITCH: Indicates whether the eNodeB needs to obtain UE information from the UE and MME in control plane CIoT EPS optimization. In control plane CIoT EPS optimization, the eNodeB needs to obtain UE capability information from the UE and requests the UE information from the MME through the Retrieve UE Information message based on the S-TMSI contained in Msg3 only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

TBS_EXTENSION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to allow for scheduling using an extended TBS. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can use the extended TBS to schedule NB2 UEs that comply with 3GPP Release 14. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB schedules UEs using non-extended TBSs as specified in 3GPP Release 13. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DL_SCHEDULING_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable downlink scheduling optimization. NB-IoT cells support downlink signaling MCS index decrease and downlink retransmission mechanism optimization only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

RU_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the RU adaptation algorithm for uplink resource scheduling. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

UCI_REP_NUM_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the UCI repetition count adaptation algorithm to adjust the number of ACK or NACK repetitions. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

SERVICE_BASED_QOS_MGMT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable service-based QoS management. If this option is selected, NB-IoT UEs allocated QCIs can be scheduled based on QoS in the cell. If this option is deselected, NB-IoT UEs cannot be scheduled based on QoS in the cell. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

CP_RRC_CONN_REESTABLISHMENT_SW: Indicates whether to allow UEs to initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode in the cell. UEs are allowed to initiate RRC connection reestablishment in control plane CIoT EPS optimization mode only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

HIGH_SPEED_MOBILITY_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support high-speed mobility. If this option is selected, the eNodeB improves the performance of high-speed UEs by mitigating the Doppler effect. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform any differentiated processing for high-speed UEs. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

MULTI_CARRIER_ENH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the multi-carrier enhancement function for NB-IoT. If this option is selected, multi-carrier enhancement is supported, and paging and random access can be performed for UEs on non-anchor carriers. If this option is deselected, multi-carrier enhancement is not supported. In this case, paging and random access can be performed for UEs only on anchor carriers. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

ADAPTIVE_PREAMBLE_TRANS_MAX_SW: Indicates whether to adaptively adjust the maximum number of preamble transmissions. If this option is selected, the eNodeB adaptively adjusts the maximum number of preamble transmissions based on the network load.
