If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not adaptively adjust the maximum number of preamble transmissions. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

PWR_CLASS_6_PWR_OFS_SCH_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to allow power offset configuration for UEs of power class 6 at cell selection or reselection and whether to optimize the downlink scheduling of UEs of power class 6. The configuration is allowed and the scheduling is optimized only if this option is selected. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

NPDCCH_OFFSET_ADAPTIVE_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable NPDCCH offset adaptation. If this option is selected, the NPDCCH offset of the UE-specific search space is adaptively configured. If this option is deselected, it is specified by the PdcchOffset parameter in the PrbPdcchCeConfig MO. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

DL_INTRF_RANDOMIZATION_SWITCH: Indicates whether to use interference randomization for NPDSCH and NPDCCH. This option applies only to NB-IoT.

100KM_CELL_RADIUS_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support cell coverage with a radius of 100 km. If this option is selected, cell coverage with a radius of 100 km is supported. If this option is deselected, cell coverage with a radius of 35 km is supported. This option applies only to NB-IoT.
Multi Op Freq Priority For Connected Mode Sw

Indicates whether to enable the policy of frequency priority for the connected mode in handovers. If this parameter is set to ON, the frequency priority for the connected mode associated with the ConnFreqPriority parameter in the RatFreqPriorityGroup MO takes effect and the frequency priority policy in handovers is supported in RAN sharing scenarios. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
SRS-Free SCC BF Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable beamforming for UEs on SCCs without SRSs. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.

NoSrsSccBfSwitch: If this option is selected, the eNodeB allows a CA UE on an SCC without SRSs to use weight information of another SCell with SRSs of the UE to enter beamforming mode. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB disables beamforming for CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs.

NoSrsSccMuBfSwitch: If this option is selected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs can be paired for MU beamforming. If this option is deselected, CA UEs on SCCs without SRSs cannot be paired for MU beamforming.
Specified User Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to specified UEs.

WBBMBB_USER_PRB_UP_LMT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB allocates PRBs to WBB or MBB UEs based on the preset upper limits of PRB usage. The uplink and downlink upper limits of PRB usage for WBB UEs are specified by the WbbUserUlPrbUpLimit and WbbUserDlPrbUpLimit parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO, respectively. The uplink and downlink upper limits of PRB usage for MBB UEs are specified by the MbbUserUlPrbUpLimit and MbbUserDlPrbUpLimit parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO, respectively. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not allocate PRBs to WBB or MBB UEs based on the preset upper limits of PRB usage. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

WBB_UE_DL_DYN_PRB_LMT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB dynamically adjusts the upper limit of PRB usage when scheduling WBB UEs in the downlink. The eNodeB dynamically adjusts the upper limit for WBB UEs based on the values of the DynCtrlUeCntLowPctThld, DynCtrlUeCntHighPctThld, and DynCtrlMbbUePrbThld parameters in the CellWttxParaCfg MO. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not dynamically adjust the upper limit of PRB usage when scheduling WBB UEs in the downlink. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
MIMO UE PA Adaptation Switch

Indicates whether to enable the MIMO UE-specific PA adaptation algorithm. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the PA adaptation algorithm takes effect. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), the algorithm does not take effect. This parameter is mainly used for peak throughput comparison test in LTE FDD. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Non-Standard Bandwidth Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable enhanced algorithms in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth.

PCFICH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable PCFICH power adaptation in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. PCFICH power adaptation is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PDCCH_POWER_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable PDCCH power optimization in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. PDCCH power optimization is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

PUCCH_SRS_ADAPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable adaptive PUCCH and SRS resource allocation in the cell with a non-standard bandwidth. Adaptive PUCCH and SRS resource allocation is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
CE Mode Handover Switch

Indicates whether to enable switching of coverage enhancement modes. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

COVERAGE_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable coverage-based switching between coverage enhancement modes.

COVERAGE_NC_CE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable coverage-based switching between NC and CE modes. This option does not apply to eMTC-only or LTE-only cells.

SERVICE_MODE_HO_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable service-based switching of coverage enhancement mode. This option does not apply to eMTC-only or LTE-only cells.
Coordination Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable coordination algorithms.

MULTI_UE_COORDINATION_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to optimize multi-user coordination. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
Measurement Optimization Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable measurement-related optimization algorithms.

GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to cancel inter-frequency measurements after a GSM measurement is triggered when UEs are performing VoIP services. If UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is selected, the eNodeB cancels all inter-frequency measurements when a GSM measurement is triggered. This reduces the GSM measurement reporting delay. If UEs are performing VoIP services and this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not cancel inter-frequency measurements when the GSM measurement is triggered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

MEAS_OBJ_PREEMPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable preemption when the number of measurement objects of a UE reaches the specified threshold. If this option is selected and the number of measurement objects of the UE reaches the specified threshold, preemption is enabled to ensure that measurements of algorithms with high priority can be delivered. If this option is deselected and the number of measurement objects of the UE reaches the specified threshold, preemption is disabled and the measurements of algorithms that are triggered later cannot be delivered. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CSFB_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to terminate inter-frequency measurements after a GSM frequency measurement starts for an E-UTRAN-to-GERAN CSFB process. If this option is selected, the eNodeB terminates all inter-frequency measurements when a GSM frequency measurement is triggered during the CSFB process, reducing the GSM frequency measurement reporting delay. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not terminate inter-frequency measurements when a GSM frequency measurement is triggered during the CSFB process. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW: Indicates whether to recover coverage-based inter-frequency measurement if a GSM measurement is terminated when the GSM_MEAS_DEL_INTERFREQ_SW option is selected. The eNodeB determines whether to deliver coverage-based inter-frequency measurement again when a GSM measurement is terminated only if the GSM_MEAS_RECOVER_INTERFREQ_SW option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SINR_MEAS_CONFIG_SW: Indicates whether to deliver downlink SINR measurement value to UEs supporting downlink SINR measurement when intra-frequency and inter-frequency periodic MDT and MR measurement events are subscribed to. The eNodeB includes the SINR measurement IE in the measurement configurations to UEs supporting downlink SINR measurement only when this option is selected and intra-frequency and inter-frequency periodic MDT and MR measurement events are subscribed to. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

LOAD_COVERAGE_MEAS_DECOUPLE_SW: Indicates whether to deliver both coverage-based measurement configurations and load-based measurement configurations. If this option is selected, the eNodeB can deliver load-based measurement configurations after it delivers coverage-based measurement configurations. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not deliver load-based measurement configurations after it delivers coverage-based measurement configurations. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

CSFB_MEAS_DEL_ACT_PCI_MEAS_SW: Indicates whether the eNodeB deletes the measurement configurations for ECGI reading previously delivered by proactive ANR-based PCI conflict detection when receiving a CSFB indication for a UE. The eNodeB deletes the measurement configuration only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Frequency Layer Switch

This parameter includes the following options: UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch, UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch, and UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch. The UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch option takes effect only when the UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch option is selected.

UtranFreqLayerMeasSwitch: If this option is selected, UTRAN frequency steering takes effect when measurement configurations are delivered for coverage- and CSFB-based handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranFreqLayerBlindSwitch: If this option is selected, UTRAN frequency steering takes effect during coverage- and CSFB-based blind handovers from E-UTRAN to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

UtranSrvccSteeringSwitch: If this option is selected, SRVCC flexible steering to UTRAN takes effect during coverage-based SRVCC to UTRAN. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
eMIMO Switch

Indicates whether to enable eMIMO-related algorithms.

Virtual4T4RSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the virtual 4T4R function. This option applies to FDD and TDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

EmimoMuMimoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the eMIMO MU-MIMO function. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

EmimoCpsSwitch: Indicates whether to enable coordinated pilot scheduling for eMIMO. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected. If this option is selected, the eNodeB determines the pilot transmission mode based on inter-cell load status.

EmimoFbaSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the following functions: fast beam alignment for eMIMO and extending UE rank reporting period in non-high-speed-railway scenarios when the CQI reporting period is adaptively changed to 40 ms. The preceding two functions are enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

New4TCodebookSwitch: Indicates whether to use the new 4T codebook. The new 4T codebook can be used for UEs that support the new 4T codebook only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

4TxTM9MuMimoSwitch: Indicates whether to enable the 4TX TM9 MU-MIMO function. This option applies only to FDD. The function is enabled only if this option is selected.

DrxSimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch: Indicates whether to allow UEs in DRX mode to send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH when SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch is set to ON and CqiMask is set to OFF. This option takes effect when the number of configured downlink transmit antennas of the cell is greater than two. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is selected, UEs in DRX mode can send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH when SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch is set to ON and CqiMask is set to OFF. If this option is deselected, the settings of the SimulAckNackAndCqiSwitch and CqiMask parameters determine whether to allow UEs in DRX mode to send CQI and ACK/NACK simultaneously on the PUCCH.

PreciseAmcSwitch: Indicates whether to enable precise AMC. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD. If this option is selected, target IBLERs are adjusted based on the interference level information exchanged with neighboring cells. If this option is deselected, the function does not take effect.

EmimoCsiEnhanceSwitch: Indicates whether to enable CSI reporting enhancement. If this option is selected, CSI reporting enhancement for eMIMO is enabled. A shorter CSI reporting period can be set for UEs running large-packet services and fast beam alignment does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is deselected, CSI reporting enhancement for eMIMO is disabled.

PUSCH12Switch: Indicates whether to enable the PUSCH 1-2 feedback mode. This option applies only to LTE FDD. If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-2 use this mode to aperiodically report CQIs. If this option is deselected, the mode cannot be used.
ROHC switch

Indicates whether to enable ROHC in the cell. If this parameter is set to ON, ROHC is enabled for UEs supporting ROHC in the cell. If this parameter is set to OFF, ROHC is disabled in the cell.

The value ON is recommended if VoIP and video services are supported in the cell.
MCPTT Switch

Indicates whether to enable the guarantee functions related to MCPTT services.

McpttQoSSwitch: Indicates whether to apply GBR to MCPTT services and prioritize scheduling for MCPTT services. GBR is applied to MCPTT services and scheduling is prioritized for MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttCongCtrlSwitch: Indicates whether to enable satisfaction-rate-based admission for MCPTT services. Satisfaction-rate-based admission is enabled for MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttRrcDrxSwitch: Indicates whether to apply a dedicated RRC connection inactivity timer and a dedicated DRX parameter group to MCPTT services. A dedicated RRC connection inactivity timer and a dedicated DRX parameter group are applied to MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

McpttVoiceMgtSwitch: Indicates whether to apply voice optimization guarantee to MCPTT services. Voice optimization guarantee is applied to MCPTT services only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
UL SU-MIMO Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable single-user MIMO (SU-MIMO) in the uplink for the cell.

ULSUMIMO2LayersSwitch: If this option is deselected, uplink SU MIMO is disabled, and single-codeword scheduling is performed for all UEs. If this option is selected, uplink SU-MIMO is enabled, and the eNodeB performs dual-codeword scheduling for SU-MIMO-capable UEs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
High Speed Inter-RAT Handover Switch

Indicates the switches used to optimize inter-RAT handover algorithms for high-speed UEs (UEs moving at a high speed) in high-speed scenarios.

HighSpeedCsfbDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional CSFB for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements or blind redirections. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HighSpeedSrvccDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional SRVCC for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements and uses a dedicated time-to-trigger in inter-RAT measurements. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

HighSpeedPsDirSwitch: Indicates whether to enable directional PS handover for high-speed UEs. When this function is enabled, an eNodeB delivers only information about inter-RAT frequencies identified as high-speed or ultra-high-speed frequencies for measurements and uses a dedicated time-to-trigger in inter-RAT measurements. This function is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
EMC Semi-Persistent Scheduling Switch

Indicates whether to use semi-persistent scheduling during talk spurts of emergency calls. If this parameter is set to OFF, dynamic scheduling is used. When this parameter is set to ON and the SpsSchSwitch option of the UlSchSwitch parameter is selected, uplink semi-persistent scheduling is used for an emergency call if the emergency call meets the conditions for activating uplink semi-persistent scheduling. When this parameter is set to ON and the SpsSchSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter is selected, downlink semi-persistent scheduling is used for an emergency call if the emergency call meets the conditions for activating downlink semi-persistent scheduling. The use of semi-persistent scheduling reduces PDCCH resource consumption. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
UL ICS RB Ratio

Indicates the maximum proportion of RBs that can be used for jointly scheduling in the uplink. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
VoLTE Path Loss Threshold for UL ICS

Indicates the threshold of path loss for selecting VoLTE UEs to preferentially perform uplink joint scheduling. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
LBS Feature Switch

This parameter is intended for the LTE cellular positioning engine feature of the service anchor.

TDOA_SWITCH: Indicates whether TDOA-based positioning is enabled. The TDoA feature takes effect only when this option is selected. The TDoA feature applies only to LampSite eNodeBs with a bandwidth of 20 MHz or 15 MHz. When this option is selected, physical cells cannot be set up on the LBBP, UBBPda, UBBPdb, or UBBPea board. Otherwise, physical cells cannot be activated. This feature must work with pRRU3911. When the CellCapacityMode parameter of an FDD cell is set to LARGECAPACITY, TDoA-based positioning is not supported. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
DTX Detection Algo Switch

BDL_PUSCH_NACK_DTX_DET_DIS_SW: Indicates whether to disable DTX identification when NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode. If this option is selected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is disabled. If this option is deselected and NACKs are transmitted on the PUSCH in ACK bundling mode, DTX identification is enabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
SAS Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable the spectrum access system (SAS) algorithm.

SAS_SPECTRUM_DYNAMIC_MGMT_SW: Indicates whether to use the SAS to perform dynamic spectrum management for the base station. If this option is selected, SAS-based dynamic spectrum management takes effect, and the MAE applies for spectrum to the SAS server and automatically configures cell parameters, including frequency, bandwidth, and transmit power, based on the application result. If this option is deselected, SAS-based dynamic spectrum management does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE TDD.
DACQ Switch

Indicates whether to enable dynamic AMBR control by QCI (DACQ). If this parameter is set to ON(On), DACQ is enabled. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), DACQ is disabled. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
UL High Order CoMP Switch

Indicates whether to enable UL high-order CoMP.

UL_HIGH_ORDER_COMP_FOR_2R: Indicates whether to enable UL high-order CoMP for 2R cells. UL high-order CoMP is enabled for 2R cells only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
Uu-based V2X Algorithm Switch

Indicates the Uu-based V2X service algorithm switch, which specifies whether to enable algorithms related to Uu-based V2X services. UU_BASED_V2X_SCH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable dedicated scheduling for Uu-based V2X services. Dedicated scheduling for Uu-based V2X services is enabled only if this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
DL Scheduling Extension Switch

Indicates whether to enable downlink scheduling algorithms for a cell. This parameter is an extension of the DlSchSwitch parameter and includes the following options:

CONGEST_VIDEO_RES_CTRL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to restrict the amount of air interface resources allocated to UEs running video services when congestion occurs. If this option is selected, the eNodeB restricts air interface resources allocated to UEs running video services when congestion occurs. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not restrict air interface resources allocated to video UEs when congestion occurs. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PTT_TBS_BASED_MCS_SEL_SWITCH: Indicates whether to select the MCS index based on the TBS for PTT voice services (with QCI 65, QCI 66, or enhanced extended QCIs). The MCS index is selected based on the TBS in downlink dynamic scheduling for PTT voice services only if this option is selected.

DL_TIME_DIM_EXT_SCH_SWITCH: Indicates whether to adopt the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. If this option is selected, the eNodeB performs the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not perform the service-based time-differentiated scheduling policy in the downlink. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

MIMO_TBSIZE_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to support the scheduling of transport blocks with TBS indexes of 26A and 33A for TM9 and TM10 UEs. The TBS indexes of 26A and 33A are supported only when this option is selected. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DL_AMBR_OPT_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable downlink AMBR optimization. If this option is selected, the eNodeB more accurately restricts the scheduling of the last packet for UEs based on AMBRs. If this option is deselected, the optimization does not take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD.

SN_COMPENSATION_HO_REEST_SW: Indicates whether the eNodeB estimates PDCP SNs at the UE side in immediate reestablishment after handovers. If this option is selected, the eNodeB estimates PDCP SNs at the UE side. The eNodeB adds an estimation amount to the minimum SN among the SNs of the PDCP packets for which an ACK is not received. The new SN is used as the starting SN after the reestablishment. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not estimate PDCP SNs received at the UE side. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

DL_COORD_SCH_EXP_OPT_SW: Indicates whether to enable coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO. If this option is selected, coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO is enabled. If this option is deselected, coordinating scheduling experience optimization for DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO is disabled. The setting of this option takes effect only if DL CoMP and inter-cell D-MIMO are enabled. This option applies only to LTE TDD.

BEST_EFFORT_GBR_SW: Indicates whether to ensure the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. If this option is selected, the eNodeB ensures the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB does not ensure the best-effort GBR for UEs with a specific QCI or SPID. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

SMART_FSS_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable smart frequency selective scheduling. If the FreqSelSwitch option of the DlSchSwitch parameter and the SMART_FSS_SWITCH option are both selected, smart frequency selective scheduling takes effect. The eNodeB dynamically adjusts the period of triggering aperiodic CQI reporting from UEs involved in frequency selective scheduling and also adjusts the validity period of aperiodically reported CQIs. Therefore, the settings of the FSUEAperCQITrigPeriod and FSUESbCQIValidityPeriod parameters are invalid in smart frequency selective scheduling. If the SMART_FSS_SWITCH option is deselected, the settings of the FSUEAperCQITrigPeriod and FSUESbCQIValidityPeriod parameters still take effect. This option applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.

PUSCH_1X_SWITCH: Indicates whether to enable the PUSCH 1-0 and PUSCH 1-1 feedback modes. These modes can be used only for short-TTI UEs. If this option is selected, UEs that support reporting on PUSCH 1-0 or PUSCH 1-1 use the corresponding mode to aperiodically report CSI. If this option is deselected, the PUSCH 1-0 and PUSCH 1-1 feedback modes cannot be used. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
MPMU Fault Detection Switch

Indicates whether to enable MPMU-level fault detection.

PRRUDETECT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the system starts to record the positions of the UE and MPMU and saves the positions in the maintenance CHR log. If this option is deselected, the system stops recording such information.
Vertical Multiple Sectors Algorithm Switch

Indicates whether to enable algorithms related to vertical multiple sectors (VMS) or massive MIMO (32T32R) cell split.

PrbBalanceSw: If this option is selected in VMS scenarios, load balancing between inner and outer cells is enabled. If this option is deselected in VMS scenarios, load balancing between inner and outer cells is disabled. If this option is selected in massive MIMO (32T32R) scenarios, load balancing between cells served by the same AAU is enabled. If this option is deselected in massive MIMO (32T32R) scenarios, load balancing between cells served by the same AAU is disabled. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
RRC Reestablishment Data Forwarding Switch

Indicates whether the eNodeB serving the cell forwards data that has not been sent to a UE in the cell (as the source cell) to the target cell when the UE experiences an RRC connection reestablishment so that the target cell can send the data to the UE. If this parameter is set to ON(On), the eNodeB forwards the data to the target cell and the target cell sends the data to the UE after the RRC connection is reestablished in the target cell. If this parameter is set to OFF(Off), the eNodeB discards the data in the preceding scenario. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Small Bandwidth Optimization Switch

Indicates the small bandwidth optimization switch, which is used to control the optimization features of small-bandwidth cells, such as 1.4 MHz, 3 MHz, and 5 MHz cell.

SMALL_BAND_RLC_OPT_SWITCH: If this option is selected, the eNodeB configures different heavy-UE number thresholds depending on the cell bandwidth configuration and performs optimization according to the settings of RlcParaAdaptSwitch and SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch. Timer parameters related to the RlcParaAdaptSwitch optimization are set to the fixed value of 200 ms. Timer parameters related to the SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch optimization are dependent on the GUI values. If this option is deselected, the eNodeB performs RLC optimization depending on the GUI values of the RlcParaAdaptSwitch and SrbRlcParaAdaptSwitch parameters. This option applies only to LTE FDD.
Idle Mode Extended DRX Switch

Indicates whether to enable idle mode eDRX for LTE UEs. For details about idle mode eDRX, see 3GPP TS 23.682. This parameter applies only to LTE FDD and LTE TDD.
Table 3 Dynamic parameters

Parameter

Description
Cell Uplink Interference Coordination Status

Indicates whether to enable uplink interference coordination. This parameter applies only to LTE TDD.
